More broadly, our results show that the emitted signals of engineered reservoirs can act as built-in failure monitors for autonomous QEC, turning rare logical faults into erasures available to a decoder and reducing fault-tolerance overhead. To this end, we develop a general framework, based on past quantum states and number-resolved master equations, to quantify the detectability of such logical failures in autonomous QEC from the emitted signal.

</details>


### [116] [Coherent states in minimal-length Quantum Mechanics: inequivalent characterizations and emergent squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01393)
*Giuseppe Gaetano Luciano,Pasquale Bosso,Daniel Chemisana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了广义不确定原理(GUP)框架下一维谐振子的相干态性质，发现标准表征方式（湮灭算符本征态、位移真空态、最小不确定波包）在存在最小长度时不再等价，并揭示了相空间轨迹的非平凡形变及内禀压缩机制等独特半经典特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论暗示普朗克尺度存在最小长度，而GUP是量子力学中编码该特征的自然方式。尽管GUP的唯象学已被广泛探索，但含最小长度的量子力学中相干态的一致表征仍然缺失，亟需系统性分析。

Method: 对一维谐振子进行系统分析，比较三种标准相干态表征（湮灭算符本征态、位移真空态、最小不确定波包）在GUP下的等价性，并研究广义相干态与GUP饱和态的动力学演化及半经典行为。

Result: 发现三种标准表征在存在最小长度时不再等价；GUP效应导致相空间轨迹发生非平凡形变；产生一种在普通量子力学中无对应物的内禀压缩机制。

Conclusion: 建立了GUP量子理论中相干性的统一框架，识别了最小长度物理的独特半经典特征，为探测量子引力效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Several approaches to quantum gravity suggest the emergence of a fundamental minimal length at the Planck scale. In quantum mechanics, this feature is naturally encoded through deformations of the Heisenberg algebra, leading to the Generalized Uncertainty Principle (GUP). While the phenomenological implications of GUP have been extensively explored, a consistent characterization of coherent states in minimal-length quantum mechanics remains elusive. In this work, we present a systematic analysis of coherent states for the one-dimensional harmonic oscillator. We show that the canonical equivalence among their standard characterizations - as eigenstates of the annihilation operator, displaced vacuum states and minimum-uncertainty wave packets - is generically lost in the presence of a minimal length. We then investigate the dynamical and semiclassical consequences of this inequivalence by comparing the evolution of generalized coherent states with that of states saturating the GUP. In particular, we demonstrate that minimal-length effects induce nontrivial deformations of phase-space trajectories and give rise to an intrinsic squeezing mechanism with no counterpart in ordinary quantum mechanics. These results establish a unified framework for coherence in GUP-based quantum theories and identify distinctive semiclassical signatures of minimal-length physics, opening a new avenue for probing quantum-gravitational effects.

</details>


### [117] [High-Precision Calibration Workflow Achieves Above $99.9\%$ CZ Gate Fidelity on a Scalable Superconducting Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01422)
*Huili Zhang,Meiling Li,Shuang Yang,Yaqing Feng,Yulong Li,Cheng Chen,Pei Liu,Guangming Xue,Haifeng Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环工作流，通过ELEA和CAFE电路抑制泄漏误差，首次在84量子比特超导处理器上实现CZ门保真度超过99.9%（相干误差仅0.007%），并在72个CZ门中取得99.25%的中位保真度，同时实现了自动化校准和长达9小时的稳定性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可扩展超导量子处理器中，缩短的相干时间引入了更多非相干误差，而有限的误差预算降低了对参数偏差导致的相干误差的容忍度，因此需要高精度的两量子比特门校准方法。

Method: 开发了闭环工作流，利用回波泄漏误差放大（ELEA）和重新利用的上下文感知保真度估计（CAFE）电路，抑制种群泄漏到非计算态，并实现CZ门的高精度校准。

Result: 在84量子比特处理器上，CZ门保真度首次超过99.9%，相干误差抑制到0.007%；在72个CZ门中获得99.25%的中位保真度；实现自动化校准，并在9小时对比监测实验中观察到增强的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为超导量子系统的量子计算建立了一条高效且自动化的路线，且在完全国产化平台上实现。

Abstract: High-fidelity universal two-qubit gates are critical for building fault-tolerant quantum computers. In scalable superconducting processors, shortened coherence times introduce more incoherent errors in gate operations. With a constrained error budget, there is reduced tolerance for coherent errors stemming from parameter deviations. In this work, we develop a closed-loop workflow to enhance the CZ gate calibration precision. Utilizing the echoed leakage error amplification (ELEA) and the repurposed context-aware fidelity estimation (CAFE) circuits, we suppress the population leakage to non-computational states, and, for the first time, demonstrate a CZ gate fidelity exceeding $99.9\%$ on an 84-qubit processor, with coherent error suppressed to $0.007\%$. Meanwhile, we obtain a median fidelity of $99.25\%$ among 72 CZ gates, demonstrating that the workflow can be generalized to the calibration of parallel CZ gates. Finally, we realize automated calibration and observe enhanced stability of the CZ gate throughout 9-hour comparative monitoring experiments. Our results, realized on a completely domestic platform, establish an efficient and automated route to quantum computation with superconducting quantum systems.

</details>


### [118] [Surface code logical operations on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01473)
*Weiping Lin,Shaojun Guo,Yuwei Ma,Zhengzhong Yi,Kai Zhang,Jiahao Bei,Jianbin Cai,Sirui Cao,Danning Chen,Guoben Chen,Jianguo Chen,Kefu Chen,Xiawei Chen,Zhe Chen,Zhiyuan Chen,Zihua Chen,Wenhao Chu,Hui Deng,Xun Ding,Zhuzhengqi Ding,Yajie Du,Bo Fan,Daojin Fan,Yuanhao Fu,Dongxin Gao,Ming Gong,Jiacheng Gui,Cheng Guo,Lianchen Han,Tan He,Linyin Hong,Yisen Hu,He-Liang Huang,Yong-Heng Huo,Chenyan Jiang,Lei Jiang,Tao Jiang,Wei Jiang,Zuokai Jiang,Dayu Li,Dongdong Li,Jiaqi Li,Jinjin Li,Junyun Li,Shaowei Li,Wei Li,Xu Li,Yuan Li,Yuhuai Li,Futian Liang,Nanxing Liao,Jin Lin,Maliang Liu,Yancheng Liu,Haoxin Lou,Kailiang Nan,Meijuan Nie,Le Niu,Wenyi Peng,Haoran Qian,Hao Rong,Tao Rong,Yanyan Shao,Huiyan Shen,Qiong Shen,Ganlin Song,Hong Su,Feifan Su,Chenyin Sun,De Sun,Liangchao Sun,Tianzuo Sun,Yingxiu Sun,Yimeng Tan,Jun Tan,Shibiao Tang,Yueyang Tang,Wenbing Tu,Jiafei Wang,Biao Wang,Chang Wang,Chen Wang,Chu Wang,Jian Wang,Rui Wang,Shengtao Wang,Xinzhe Wang,Zhi Wang,Zuolin Wei,Gang Wu,Yulin Wu,Hongjun Xia,Shouzhong Xia,Shiyong Xie,Zhilin Xie,Liang Xiong,Jianping Xu,Yan Xu,Yu Xu,Chun Xue,Kai Yan,Xin Yan,Weifeng Yang,Xinpeng Yang,Yang Yang,Yicheng Yang,Yangsen Ye,Zhenping Ye,Jianghan Yin,Chong Ying,Jiale Yu,Qinjing Yu,Chen Zha,Shaoyu Zhan,Cha Zhang,Fang Zhang,Haibin Zhang,He Zhang,Huanmei Zhang,Kaili Zhang,Qipeng Zhang,Shijia Zhang,Wen Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Yongzhuo Zhang,Ziying Zhang,Guming Zhao,Xintao Zhao,Youwei Zhao,Zhong Zhao,Luyuan Zheng,Fei Zhou,Liang Zhou,Naibin Zhou,Chengjun Zhu,Qingling Zhu,Yongsheng Zhu,Guihong Zou,Haonan Zou,Qiang Zhang,Chao-Yang Lu,Jianxin Chen,Cheng-Zhi Peng,Fusheng Chen,XiaoBo Zhu,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在107量子比特超导处理器上，实验实现了基于补丁的表面码逻辑处理关键元素，包括合并/分裂、扩展/收缩、畴壁/扭结缺陷介导的变形等原语操作，并组合实现了逻辑态路由、CNOT门、Hadamard门和相位门（构成克利福德生成集），所有操作在距离三的旋转表面码补丁上通过多轮综合征提取和神经网络解码完成，无需后选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要在不破坏量子纠错保护的前提下操作编码信息。在平面表面码架构中，码形变和晶格手术为此提供了基于局域测量的方案，但此前缺乏实验验证。

Method: 在107量子比特超导处理器上，实现可复用的原语层（合并/分裂、补丁扩展/收缩、畴壁和扭结缺陷介导的形变），组合这些原语实现逻辑态路由、CNOT门、Hadamard门和相位门，采用距离三旋转表面码补丁、多轮综合征提取和神经网络解码。

Result: 成功实现了所有克利福德生成集逻辑门操作，所有操作无需后选择，在超导表面码实验中从受保护逻辑存储推进到主动的基于补丁的容错逻辑操作。

Conclusion: 该工作将超导表面码实验从受保护逻辑存储推进到主动的、基于补丁的容错逻辑操作，为可扩展容错量子计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires logical operations that manipulate encoded information while preserving quantum error-correction protection. In planar surface-code architectures, code deformation and lattice surgery provide a local, measurement-based route to such operations. Here we experimentally realize key elements of patch-based surface-code logical processing on a 107-qubit superconducting quantum processor. We first implement a reusable primitive layer comprising merge and split, patch expansion and shrinkage, and deformations mediated by domain walls and twist defects. We then compose these primitives to realize logical state routing, the logical controlled-NOT gate, and the single-qubit Hadamard and phase gates, which together form a Clifford-generating set. All operations are implemented on distance-three rotated surface-code patches with multi-round syndrome extraction and neural-network decoding, without post-selection. Our results advance superconducting surface-code experiments from protected logical memory to active, patch-based fault-tolerant logical operations.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum mutual information as a robust probe of integrability in open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02462)
*Nirupam Sen,Keshav Das Agarwal,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Haar平均总关联和(aSTC)及平均真多体纠缠作为量子信息加扰的探针，在长程量子XYZ自旋模型中证明aSTC的时间涨落可以作为可积与混沌动力学的可靠指标，并在开放系统中研究了马尔可夫与非马尔可夫环境下aSTC与OTOC的区分能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的动力学编码了哈密顿量是否可积或混沌的特征，现有加扰探测手段（如OTOC）存在局限性，需要发展更鲁棒的量子信息加扰探针。

Method: 建立基于Haar平均总关联和(aSTC)与平均真多体纠缠的信息论框架，在长程量子XYZ自旋模型中（可积极限为近邻横场XY模型）进行分析；同时考虑与热库耦合的马尔可夫与非马尔可夫环境。

Result: aSTC的长时间平均和时间涨落可以作为系统大小无关的可积与混沌动力学签名；在马尔可夫环境中仅中间时间有效；非马尔可夫环境中信息回流恢复加扰动力学；在弱马尔可夫振幅阻尼和非马尔可夫退相位噪声下，aSTC在OTOC失效时仍能区分可积性。

Conclusion: aSTC是一种比OTOC更鲁棒的量子信息加扰探针，尤其在弱马尔可夫噪声环境中具有独特优势。

Abstract: The dynamics of a quantum system encode signatures of whether the underlying Hamiltonian is integrable or chaotic, giving rise to the concept of quantum information scrambling through the properties of the resulting dynamical states or operators. We introduce an information-theoretic framework based on the Haar-averaged sum of total correlations (aSTC), together with average genuine multipartite entanglement generated dynamically from initially fully separable states, as robust probes of quantum information scrambling. Using the long-range quantum XYZ spin model in transverse and longitudinal magnetic fields, whose integrable limit is the nearest-neighbor transverse XY model, we demonstrate that the long-time average and, more importantly, the temporal fluctuations of the aSTC provide a faithful and system-size-independent signature of integrable and chaotic dynamics, similar to the conventional measure of scrambling, out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC). When the system is in contact with the thermal reservoir and system-bath coupling follows Markovianity, we find that the fluctuations of the aSTC and OTOC continue to distinguish integrable and chaotic dynamics only at intermediate times. However, we observe that in the non-Markovian domain, information backflow restores the scrambling dynamics, enabling the aSTC to retain its distinguishing power even at long times. Interestingly, we exhibit that, under Markovian amplitude damping and non-Markovian dephasing noise, the temporal fluctuations of the aSTC can discriminate between integrability and non-integrability in the weak Markovian regime, even when OTOC fails to do so.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum nonlocal correlations of anomalous weak values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01491)
*Ron Cohen,Avshalom C. Elitzur,Eliahu Cohen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了CHSH算子在预选和后选(PPS)量子系综中的反常弱值，将其视为后选条件下Bell型非局域关联的广义界限。研究发现后选和纠缠是不同的操作资源：后选可单独增强关联，但纠缠是超越对应可分PPS界限的必要条件，二者结合可获得最强关联。非局域弱值可作为后选纠缠见证，在理想状态自适应设定下能检测所有具有非零并发度的纯两量子比特源状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后选(post-selection)和纠缠(entanglement)作为两种不同量子资源在增强非局域关联中的各自作用和相互关系，以及非局域弱值作为纠缠检测工具的可能性。

Method: 通过分析PPS量子系综中CHSH算子的弱值，在固定预选和后选态重叠的条件下，比较三种场景：无限制边界态、一个可分边界态、两个边界态均可分。分别推导每种情况下的最大弱值和经过所有CHSH算子优化后的最大界限。

Result: 后选和纠缠是不同操作资源；后选可单独增强关联，但纠缠是超越可分PPS界限的必要条件；超越可分界限的增强与优化态的并发度密切相关；非局域弱值可作为后选纠缠见证，能检测所有具有非零并发度的纯两量子比特源状态。

Conclusion: 后选和纠缠结合可产生最强关联，非局域弱值在标准CHSH纠缠测试无法判定的情况下仍能检测纠缠。这为结合后选和纠缠的混合协议提供了理论基础，潜在应用包括改进量子传感、弱值放大和量子信息处理。

Abstract: Violations of Bell inequalities are a hallmark of entanglement, with only entangled states capable of exceeding classical bounds in standard Bell tests. Here we analyze anomalous weak values of the CHSH operator in pre- and post-selected (PPS) quantum ensembles, treating them as generalized bounds on Bell-type nonlocal correlations in the presence of post-selection. Fixing the overlap between the pre- and post-selected states, we compare three scenarios: unrestricted boundary states, one separable boundary state, and both boundary states separable. For each case, we derive both the maximal weak value for a fixed Bell operator and the maximal bound obtained by further optimizing over all CHSH operators. Our results show that post-selection and entanglement are distinct operational resources: post-selection alone can enhance correlations, but entanglement is necessary to exceed the corresponding separable PPS bounds, and their combination yields the strongest attainable correlations. We further show that the enhancement beyond the separable bound closely tracks the concurrence of the states that optimize the bounds, identifying entanglement as the source of the additional correlation strength. Finally, we show that nonlocal weak values provide post-selected entanglement witnesses, and we give a constructive protocol that detects every pure two-qubit source state with nonzero concurrence in the ideal state-adapted setting, even in regimes where the corresponding standard CHSH entanglement test is inconclusive. In this state-adapted setting, we explicitly construct the post-selection and CHSH measurements that achieve the largest possible separation from the separable PPS bound. More broadly, our results motivate hybrid protocols that combine post-selection and entanglement, with possible applications to improved quantum sensing, weak-value amplification, and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [121] [Symmetry conservation with Trotterization and Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01560)
*Edith Leal-Sánchez,Fanny Vain,Jong-Kwon Ha,Ryan J. MacDonell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用厄米激发算符表达哈密顿量，使其映射为对易泡利串之和，从而保持阿贝尔对称性；对非阿贝尔对称性则通过“算子剪纸术”进行正交投影和幺正旋转处理。测试表明，对称性保持的算子池在小分子中误差较大，但随分子增大而减小，且二阶Trotter化可消除误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotter化导致的误差会破坏哈密顿量的对称性，而现有方法通常不保证对称性守恒，因此需要发展既能高效量子实现又能保持对称性的哈密顿量分解方法。

Method: 1) 将哈密顿量用厄米激发算符表示，使其映射为对易泡利串之和；2) 对阿贝尔对称性群，该分解天然守恒对称性；3) 对非阿贝尔对称性群，提出“算子剪纸术”（operator kirigami），通过正交投影切割非对易算符之和，并用幺正旋转折叠项。

Result: 在小分子和基组上测试发现：电子数和自旋对称性守恒的算子池导致更大误差，但误差随分子增大而减小；二阶Trotter化可抵消该误差。

Conclusion: 展示了在经典计算机上通过电子结构理论中对称性守恒工具测试量子计算算法的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for quantum chemistry and other many-body fermionic systems work by expressing the Hamiltonian in a basis of qubits and fragmenting the Hamiltonian into a sum of products of Pauli operators whose exponentials are easily encoded on a quantum device. Applying the product of exponentials, known as Trotterization, leads to an error associated with the non-commutativity of operators. This error can lead to breaking the symmetries of the Hamiltonian because the fragments are not symmetry conserving in general. Nonetheless, many algorithms for time evolution rely on Trotterization, including time evolution and quantum phase estimation. We show that we can express the Hamiltonian in terms of Hermitian excitation operators which map to sums of commuting Pauli strings for any encoding and conserve symmetries corresponding to Abelian groups of symmetry operators. Symmetries corresponding to non-Abelian groups, on the other hand, are not fully conserved by Trotterized Hermitian excitation operators, so we developed ``operator kirigami'' to cut the sum of non-commuting operators by orthogonal projection and to fold terms together using unitary rotations. We tested pools of operators for small molecules and basis sets, and found that electron number and spin symmetry conserving pools led to greater errors that decreased for larger molecules and were negated with second-order Trotterization. Our work shows the potential for testing quantum computing algorithms on classical computers by adapting tools used in electronic structure theory with conserved symmetries.

</details>


### [122] [An Information-Theoretic Principle for Optimal Quantum Encoding: Tight Frames and Equiangular Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01564)
*Farhad Farokhi,Shuixin Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度研究了经典数据的最优量子编码策略，证明了量子计算推理准确率的上界由最大量子泄漏决定，并指出纯态、基编码（高维）和相位编码（低维）是最优策略，其中等角紧框架（含SIC-POVMs）是最优对称编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索经典数据在量子辅助统计推断中的最优编码策略，寻找一种任务无关的通用质量度量来指导量子编码设计。

Method: 采用信息论方法，定义并分析最大量子泄漏（maximal quantum leakage）作为编码质量的通用度量；通过理论推导证明上界关系；利用纯态、基编码、相位编码及等角紧框架（ETF）等工具刻画最优编码结构。

Result: 证明了任何量子推理过程的准确率受最大量子泄漏上界约束；最大量子泄漏是任务无关的通用质量度量；纯态可实现最优通用编码；高维时基编码最优，低维时相位编码最优；紧框架（特别是ETF）是低维最优对称编码，SIC-POVMs为特例。

Conclusion: 建立了量子泄漏作为编码质量通用度量的理论框架，给出了不同维度和条件下的最优编码策略，为量子统计推断的编码设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Optimal encoding of classical data for quantum-assisted statistical inference is investigated from an information-theoretic perspective. We prove that the accuracy of any quantum-computing inference procedure is upper bounded by the maximal quantum leakage from the classical data through its quantum encoding, establishing leakage as a universal, task-agnostic quality measure for encoders. This demonstrates that the maximal quantum leakage is a universal measure of the quality of the encoding strategy for statistical inference as it only depends on the quantum encoding of the data and not the inference task itself. The optimal universal encoding strategy, i.e., an encoding strategy that maximizes the maximal quantum leakage, is proved to be attained by pure states. When there are enough qubits, basis encoding is proved to be universally optimal. However, when the dimension of the system is small, phase encoding is optimal. For the latter, any tight frame, any ensemble whose average state is the maximally mixed state, is in fact optimal. Within tight frames, equiangular tight frames (ETFs) are distinguished as the uniquely symmetric optimal encodings, i.e., they saturate the Welch lower bound on pairwise overlaps and possess a self-referential optimal measurement. Prominent special cases are the qubit trine, the regular simplex, and symmetric informationally complete positive operator-valued measures (SIC-POVMs), for which the ETF structure and explicit codeword constructions are provided. Numerical examples are presented to validate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [123] [A Validation Framework for Quantum Simulation of Spin Dynamics against Inelastic Neutron Scattering and Classical Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01568)
*Gilles Buchs,Elaine Wong,Anshumitra Baul,Kathleen E. Hamilton,Arnab Banerjee,Stephan Eidenbenz,Gábor B. Halász,Keerthi Kumaran,Thomas Maier,Thomas Naughton,Elijah Pelofske,Vincent Russo,Allen Scheie,Yigit Subasi,D. Alan Tennant,Akram Touil,Travis S. Humble,Andrew T. Sornborger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对量子模拟动力学自旋响应的跨管道验证框架，通过非弹性中子散射和经典多体模拟作为实验和计算锚点，利用正向/逆观测映射、不确定性传播、鲁棒性测试和分层度量族，实现对量子模拟在随机不确定性和结构失真下的系统性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟的动力学自旋响应的定量验证面临根本性挑战：实验、经典模拟和量子模拟产生的原生可观测物理量各不相同。随着量子模拟协议从理论走向硬件级基准测试（与中子散射数据对标），且未来目标是验证可能经典难解的区域，亟需一个统一的跨管道验证框架。

Method: 提出跨管道验证框架，包含：（1）显式的正向和逆观测映射；（2）基于协方差或重采样的不确定性传播；（3）针对结构化失真的鲁棒性测试；（4）互补度量族的分层结构。框架将随机不确定性与鲁棒性导致的失真区分开，并引入致动器感知的反馈逻辑以改善一致性而不掩盖失真的物理来源。

Result: 建立了能够在管道级、求解器级和模型级进行分层验证的框架，通过度量级的不确定性和失真信息支持验证决策；引入致动器感知反馈逻辑以提升实验与模拟之间的一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子模拟的定量验证提供了系统性方法论，未来可扩展至上游不确定性和失真建模、自适应反馈、超越全经典基准的不对称验证、容错工作流以及社区基础设施构建。

Abstract: Quantitative validation of quantum simulations of dynamical spin response remains challenging because experiment, classical simulation, and quantum simulation do not produce the same native observables. This problem has become increasingly important as quantum simulation protocols for dynamical response have progressed from theory to hardware-level benchmarking against neutron-scattering data, while the longer term goal is validation in regimes that may eventually become classically intractable, including in future fault-tolerant implementations. Here, we develop a cross-pipeline validation framework for quantum simulation, using inelastic neutron scattering and classical many-body simulation as complementary experimental and computational anchors, based on explicit forward and inverse observable maps, covariance- or resampling-based uncertainty propagation, robustness tests for structured distortion, and a hierarchy of complementary metric families. The framework distinguishes stochastic uncertainty from robustness-induced distortion, carries both explicitly through the comparison chain, and uses the resulting metric-level uncertainty and distortion information to support layered validation at the pipeline, solver, and model levels. We also introduce actuator-aware feedback logic aimed at improving agreement without obscuring the physical origin of any remaining mismatch. We close by outlining future extensions of this methodology, including upstream uncertainty and distortion modeling, adaptive feedback, asymmetric validation beyond full classical benchmarking, fault-tolerant workflows, and community infrastructure for reproducible validation.

</details>


### [124] [Structured Factorization Approaches for Quantum State Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01608)
*Zhen Qin,Joseph M. Lukens,Brian T. Kirby,Zhihui Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化分解的统一框架，通过将密度矩阵参数化为$FF^\dagger$并对因子$F$施加结构化约束，实现可扩展的量子态层析成像。该框架涵盖Cholesky分解、低秩矩阵、矩阵乘积算子及神经密度算子等多种结构先验，并提出了基于投影梯度下降和幂法的高效优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析成像的复杂度随系统规模指数增长，需要利用低秩性、张量网络结构、神经网络表示等先验知识来降低样本复杂度和参数复杂度。现有方法各自独立，缺乏统一的处理框架。

Method: 提出结构化分解框架，将密度矩阵参数化为$FF^\dagger$，其中因子$F$属于某种结构化模型类（如低秩矩阵、矩阵乘积算子、MLP/Transformer等神经密度算子）。基于此框架，将QST转化为因子空间上的优化问题，提出投影梯度下降法和基于幂法的步长无关快速收敛算法。

Result: 建立了广泛结构化量子态类的最小二乘估计样本复杂度的统一统计分析；提出的投影梯度下降法适用于多种结构化参数化和重构目标；导出的幂法无需步长参数，具有快速收敛特性，在因子无约束情况下恢复为Cover方法。

Conclusion: 结构化分解框架为可扩展量子态层析成像提供了统一的理论和算法基础，能够灵活整合多种结构先验，并保证密度矩阵的物理有效性。

Abstract: Since the complexity of quantum state tomography (QST) scales exponentially with system size, exploiting priors such as low-rankness, tensor-network structures, and neural-network representations is essential for scalable QST in terms of sample complexity and parameter complexity. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework, termed structured factorization, that builds on BurerMonteiro-type factorization by parametrizing the density matrix as $FF^\dagger$, where the factor $F$ is constrained to belong to a structured model class. This factorization guarantees physical validity by construction while allowing a broad range of structural priors to be incorporated directly through the choice of the factor space, ranging from the generic Cholesky decomposition to low-rank matrices, matrix product operators, and neural density operators based on multilayer perceptron and transformer architectures. Building on this structured factorization framework, we formulate QST as an optimization problem over the factor space from measurement data. We first develop a unified statistical analysis of the sample complexity of least-squares estimation for a broad class of structured quantum states. We then propose a projected gradient descent method that operates directly on the factor space and accommodates a wide range of structural parametrizations and reconstruction objectives. To further exploit the geometry of the maximum-likelihood estimation formulation and the constraints on the factors, we derive a power method that yields a step-size-free algorithm with fast convergence, recovering Covers method as a special case when the factor is unconstrained.

</details>


### [125] [Noise Suppression via Pulsed All-Optical Magnetometry with Nitrogen-Vacancy Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01615)
*Xiechen Zheng,Jeyson Támara-Isaza,John W. Blanchard,Ronald L. Walsworth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种脉冲式全光学（AO）磁力测定协议，通过在一个光脉冲内进行两次光致发光（PL）测量来抑制共模噪声，在近零磁场下实现了比连续波（cw）AO技术最高10倍的低频噪声改善，并研究了PL读取时序和光脉冲间暗时间对AO-PL对比度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续波（cw）全光学磁力测定方法易受光强噪声等系统波动影响，限制了其性能。为克服这一限制，需要开发能抑制共模噪声的新型协议。

Method: 提出脉冲式全光学协议，采用单个光脉冲内两次PL测量来抑制共模噪声；在近零磁场下实验验证该协议可分辨NV-NV交叉弛豫产生的AO-PL对比度特征。

Result: 脉冲AO协议相比传统cw AO技术，低频噪声底噪最高提升10倍；AO-PL对比度依赖于PL读取时序和光脉冲间暗时间τ，而最优τ随NV浓度变化。

Conclusion: 该研究为优化NV-diamond样品以实现高效全光学操作提供了指导，具有简化实验设计和拓宽样品兼容性的潜力。

Abstract: All-optical (AO) microwave-free magnetometry using nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond simplifies experimental design and broadens sample compatibility. While continuous-wave (cw) detection of AO photoluminescence (PL) changes is commonly employed, its performance is susceptible to systematic fluctuations such as optical intensity noise. To address these challenges, we introduce a pulsed AO protocol that employs two PL measurements within an optical pulse to suppress common-mode noise. At near-zero magnetic field, we experimentally demonstrate that the pulsed AO protocol resolves AO-PL contrast features arising from NV-NV cross-relaxation, achieving up to 10$\times$ improvement in the low-frequency noise floor compared to conventional cw AO techniques. We further investigate the dependence of AO-PL contrast on PL readout timing and the dark time duration $τ$ between optical pulses, with the optimal $τ$ varying based on NV concentrations. These findings provide insights into optimizing NV-diamond samples for effective AO operation across diverse applications.

</details>


### [126] [Chiral interaction enhanced magnon bundle emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01635)
*Zhicai Chen,Deyi Kong,Chengdeng Gou,Xiangming Hu,Fei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在手性级联腔中放置量子比特和磁子，利用单向相互作用延长目标激发态寿命、抑制磁子再激发，从而提升双磁子束流的纯度和数量，实现高质量的多磁子源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁子束流发射方案中，磁子再激发会降低多磁子源的质量（纯度与数量），亟需一种有效抑制再激发、提升多磁子发射性能的方法。

Method: 将量子比特和磁子分别置于级联腔中，引入手性（单向）相互作用方案，通过分析系统动力学和激发态寿命来评估磁子束流发射性能。

Result: 手性单向相互作用有效延长了目标激发态的寿命，抑制了磁子再激发，从而显著提升了双磁子束流的平均纯度和数量。

Conclusion: 手性相互作用不仅能实现方向性控制，还能提高多磁子源的质量，有望应用于量子信息处理中的高质量多磁子源构建。

Abstract: In this paper, we suggest a chiral interaction scheme to enhance magnon bundle emission by placing a qubit and a magnon into a cascaded-cavity setup, respectively. It is found that the unidirectional interaction prolongs the lifetime of the target excited state, thereby suppressing the magnon re-excitation and promoting both the average purity and number of two-magnon bundles. Consequently, the chiral interaction not only offers directional control but also improves the quality of the multi-magnon source, which may find potential applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [127] [Bayesian Monotone Metrics for Multiparameter Quantum Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01685)
*Jianchao Zhang,Koichi Yamagata,Jun Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了贝叶斯单调度量（Bayesian monotone metrics）框架，通过将Petz单调度量应用于先验平均态，为多参数贝叶斯量子估计提供了统一的几何表述，生成了一系列可计算的贝叶斯风险下界，并且证明了这些下界普遍优于对应的量子van Trees界，且优化后比贝叶斯SLD和RLD界更紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数贝叶斯量子估计一直缺乏统一的几何表述，现有的贝叶斯Cramér-Rao（量子van Trees）界在有限数据场景下不够紧致，需要一种能自然纳入测量不相容性且可计算的下界框架。

Method: 通过将Petz单调度量作用于先验平均态，引入贝叶斯单调度量；构造量子贝叶斯后验均值算子和量子贝叶斯对偶Fisher信息矩阵；对所有算子单调函数进行优化，证明其可坍缩为单参数子族。

Result: 建立了一族可计算的贝叶斯风险下界，证明了每个单调度量对应的下界普遍优于量子van Trees界；优化后的下界在代表性例子中严格优于贝叶斯SLD和RLD界。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯单调度量统一了多参数贝叶斯量子估计的信息几何视角，为系统性地计算性能极限提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Bayesian quantum estimation offers a finite-data framework for quantum sensing and metrology, yet a unified geometric formulation for multiparameter Bayes risk has been lacking. We introduce Bayesian monotone metrics by evaluating Petz monotone metrics on the prior-averaged state, providing a Bayesian extension of the full class of statistically meaningful (CPTP) quantum metrics. This framework yields Bayesian quantities, including quantum posterior-mean operators and a quantum Bayesian dual Fisher-information matrix, and it leads to a systematic family of computable lower bounds on the Bayes risk. The resulting bounds naturally incorporate multiparameter measurement incompatibility and, for every monotone metric in the family, we prove a universal dominance over the corresponding quantum van Trees (Bayesian Cramér--Rao) bound. Moreover, we show that optimizing over all operator monotone functions collapses to a one-parameter subfamily, turning the tightest bound into a tractable optimization with a clear geometric interpretation. In representative examples, the optimized bounds are strictly tighter than the Bayesian SLD and RLD bounds. Our results establish Bayesian monotone metrics as a unifying information-geometric perspective on Bayesian quantum estimation, enabling systematic and computable performance limits in multiparameter settings.

</details>


### [128] [Performance of a two-mode coherent superposed channel in continuous-variable quantum teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01786)
*Deepak,Arpita Chatterjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入双模叠加算符 $A=tab+ra^\dagger b^\dagger$，作用于双模相干态产生双模相干叠加量子态，利用Wigner分布和Wigner对数负性研究其非经典性和量子非高斯性，并将其作为纠缠资源应用于Braunstein-Kimble连续变量隐形传态协议，分析了非经典性与非高斯性对传态保真度的影响，找到了超越经典阈值的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索双模相干叠加态的非经典性和非高斯性，并阐明这些量子特性在连续变量量子信息处理（特别是量子隐形传态）中的操作意义。

Method: 构造双模叠加算符 $A=tab+ra^\dagger b^\dagger$ 作用于双模相干态，通过Wigner分布和Wigner对数负性（WLN）量化非经典性与非高斯性，并评估该态作为纠缠资源在Braunstein-Kimble CV隐形传态协议中对相干态和压缩态输入的理想传态保真度。

Result: 特定参数条件下，增强的非高斯特征或增加的非经典性能够使传态保真度超越经典阈值。

Conclusion: 工程化双模量子态的非经典性和非高斯性对提升连续变量量子信息处理性能具有操作意义。

Abstract: Glauber's coherent state is denoted by $\ketα$ and its two-mode extension is represented by $\ket{α,β}$. In this work, we introduce a two-mode superposition operator $A=tab+ra^\dagger b^\dagger$, whose action on the two-mode coherent state produces the two-mode coherent superposed quantum state $\ketψ=(tab+ra^\dagger b^\dagger)\ket{α,β}$. We investigate the nonclassicality and quantum non-Gaussianity of this state by means of the Wigner distribution and Wigner logarithmic negativity. Once its intrinsic nonclassical and non-Gaussian structure is established, the state is employed as the entangled resource in the Braunstein-Kimble continuous-variable (CV) teleportation protocol. We compute the ideal teleportation fidelity for coherent and squeezed inputs and analyze how the strengths of nonclassicality and non-Gaussianity influence the teleportation efficiency. Our results identify specific parameter regimes where enhanced non-Gaussian features or increased nonclassicality enable fidelities beyond the classical threshold, thereby revealing the operational significance of engineered two-mode quantum states in CV quantum information processing.

</details>


### [129] [Structure-Aware Compilation for Scalable Neutral-Atom Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01787)
*Dekuan Dong,Fengyu Zou,Hengzhun Chen,Guorui Zhu,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维中性原子阵列上结构化量子门家族的编译问题，通过代数分解（单量子门）和图论调度（C-Z门）方法，在真实硬件约束下大幅减少寻址层数和原子传输操作。实验表明，单量子门寻址层数最高减少50%，C-Z门传输操作减少约50%，QAOA电路传输成本降低超30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维中性原子量子计算硬件中，寻址和原子传输是主要的调度瓶颈。现有方法缺乏对结构化门家族的代数与图论结构的系统利用，导致硬件资源利用率低。本文旨在将物理约束转化为可求解的数学问题，从而优化编译效率。

Method: 对于单量子门，利用矩阵层面的代数结构实现秩一分解，减少寻址层数；对于C-Z门，将传输调度问题建模为图论问题，设计高效的编译算法，并给出可证明的性能保证。

Result: 单量子门寻址层数相比朴素行/列实现减少最多50%；C-Z门原子传输操作减少约50%；QAOA for MaxCut电路中传输成本降低超过30%。

Conclusion: 中性原子硬件的物理约束可转化为代数与图论结构，将硬件调度瓶颈转化为可处理的分解与着色问题，验证了该方法在实际量子电路编译中的有效性。

Abstract: We study the compilation of structured quantum gate families on two-dimensional neutral-atom arrays, aiming to reduce addressing and transport overhead under realistic hardware constraints. For single-qubit gates, we exploit the algebraic structures of gate families at the matrix level, enabling efficient rank-one decompositions over appropriate algebraic structures and thereby reducing the number of addressing layers. For controlled-Z (C-Z) gates, we formulate the transport scheduling problem using graph-theoretic models, leading to efficient compilation algorithms under realistic transport constraints. We provide provable performance guarantees for the proposed methods and validate them through extensive numerical experiments. Across representative single-qubit gate families, our methods reduce the number of addressing layers by up to a factor of two compared with naïve row- or column-wise implementations. For C-Z gates, our scheduling strategy reduces the required number of atom transport operations by approximately 50\%. When applied to QAOA circuits for MaxCut, the proposed framework reduces transport cost by more than 30\% on average. These results show that the physical constraints of neutral-atom hardware can be converted into algebraic and graph-theoretic structure, turning a hardware-level scheduling bottleneck into tractable decomposition and coloring problems.

</details>


### [130] [Interferometric characterization of the relative phase between two X-ray free-electron laser pulses using long-lived Mössbauer resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01796)
*Lukas Wolff,Jörg Evers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Mössbauer共振的Ramsey型干涉测量方法，用于测量高重复频率X射线自由电子激光（XFEL）变换极限脉冲序列间的相对相位，从而为高阶相干光谱学实验提供关键相位信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相干的光谱方法是探索物质结构与动力学的有力工具，但在向更高光子能量推进时，如何生成具有良好表征的相对延迟和相位的脉冲序列仍是一项挑战。

Method: 在XFEL束路中（初级实验的上游或下游）引入长寿命Mössbauer共振，利用其桥接XFEL脉冲间的时间间隔，进行Ramsey型干涉测量，从而提取后续变换极限脉冲之间的相对相位。

Result: 该方法能够测量高重复频率XFEL脉冲序列间的相对相位 φ，并将该相位作为额外输入用于初级实验的数据分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的相位测量方法解决了高光子能量下相干光谱学中的关键难题，有望推动XFEL在超快结构动力学和量子相干控制等领域的应用。

Abstract: Coherence-based spectroscopy methods are powerful tools to explore structure and dynamics of matter. However, towards higher photon energies, the generation of sequences of pulses with well-characterized relative delays and phases remains a challenge. Here, we introduce a method to measure the relative phase $\varphi$ between subsequent transform-limited pulses from high-repetition-rate x-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs). It is based on a Ramsey-type interference measurement, enabled by introducing long-lived Mössbauer resonances into the XFEL beam path up- or downstream a primary experiment, which allow one to bridge the temporal gap between the XFEL pulses. The measured phase can be used as additional input for the analysis of the primary experiment.

</details>


### [131] [Extracting Work from Discrete Quantum Polytropic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01811)
*Vishal Anand,Swarup Kumar Giri,Avijit Misra,Subhadip Mitra,Samyadeb Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究为时间依赖、非马尔可夫量子热机建立了可提取功的上界，通过光机循环证明最大效率需要准静态操作以利用非马尔可夫共振，而最大功率则要求有限时间 regime，且Trotter化误差会抑制量子记忆效应导致退化为马尔可夫Otto极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立时间依赖、非马尔可夫量子热机在有限浴条件下的可提取功上界，并解析分离系统-浴关联、浴非平衡态和残余相互作用能各自的热力学代价。

Method: 通过解析推导建立功的上界，并通过量子多方腔光机械循环进行操作性评估。

Result: 最大效率需要准静态操作以利用相干非马尔可夫共振；最大功率要求有限时间 regime；Trotter化误差表现为物理噪声，抑制量子记忆效应导致退化为马尔可夫Otto极限；有限浴相互作用的切换带来永久性能量税。

Conclusion: 量子记忆资源的利用与有限功率运行分属不同操作 regime，两者不可兼得。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound on extractable work for time-dependent, non-Markovian quantum heat engines operating with finite baths. This bound analytically isolates the distinct thermodynamic penalties arising from system-bath correlations, bath non-equilibrium, and residual interaction energy. Evaluating this framework operationally via a quantum polytropic cavity-optomechanical cycle, we demonstrate that maximal efficiency requires quasi-static operation to successfully harvest coherent, non-Markovian system-bath resonances. Conversely, optimising for maximum power enforces a strict finite-time regime. Under realistic hardware constraints, this acceleration necessitates larger discrete operational steps, where we expect Trotterisation errors to manifest as physical noise. Such noise would irreversibly suppress delicate quantum memory effects, forcing a collapse to the memoryless Markovian Otto limit. Coupled with the permanent energetic tax of switching finite-bath interactions, our results indicate that the exploitation of quantum memory resources and finite-power operation belong to different operational regimes.

</details>


### [132] [Memory Device for Photons by exploiting Brillouin Interactions in Nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01816)
*Hashem Zoubi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用纳米光纤中的受激布里渊散射实现单光子级光存储的可能性。首先介绍了通过相干缓冲将光信号相干转移到声波的标准方法，但声波寿命限制了其在单光子信号中的应用。随后提出了一种无增益无损耗的慢光配置，利用两个反向传播泵浦场的光子-声子布里渊相互作用，通过纳米线中的显著慢光时延实现光子存储，并讨论了抑制热声子散射影响的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子存储器是量子信息处理和量子通信的关键器件，现有方法在纳米尺度波导中实现单光子级光存储仍面临挑战，特别是声波寿命对存储时间的限制以及热噪声的影响。

Method: 标准方法：利用受激布里渊散射在纳米波导中通过相干缓冲将光信号相干转移到声波，再通过逆过程提取。改进方法：引入双泵浦反向传播场配置，利用光子-声子布里渊相互作用实现无增益无损耗的慢光传播，通过纳米线中显著的时间延迟实现光子存储。

Result: 标准方法中声波寿命限制了单光子信号的适用性；改进配置可实现单光子级别的慢光信号而无增益或损耗；确定了抑制热声子散射影响所需的条件。

Conclusion: 通过布里渊相互作用在纳米光纤中实现单光子级光存储在理论上是可行的，改进配置克服了标准方法中声波寿命的限制，但热声子散射是需要关注的关键噪声来源。

Abstract: Memory devices for single photons are notable components for quantum information processing and quantum communications. The present study investigates the possibility of achieving storage of light at the level of single photons inside nanofibers by exploiting stimulated Brillouin scattering. We present first the standard approach using a coherent buffer in a nanoscale waveguide by transferring the optical signal coherently to an acoustic wave, and that can be extracted by the reverse process. The life time of the acoustic wave put limitation on the applicability of such approach for single photon signals. We introduce a configuration for achieving a slow signal at the level of single photons without gain or loss. The process utilizes photon-phonon Brillouin interactions involving two counter propagating pump fields. The photon storage is achieved through time delay of significantly slow signal inside nanowires. We address the condition for getting negligible influence due to the scattering off thermal phonons.

</details>


### [133] [Low-ancilla block encodings via Hamiltonian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01843)
*Yuxin Zhang,Changpeng Shao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义量子信号处理的单辅助比特块编码构造方法，通过哈密顿量演化实现算符的近似块编码。使用高阶Trotterization时仅需一个辅助比特，电路深度为$\widetilde O(L(\alpha/\varepsilon)^{o(1)})$；使用多乘积公式时电路深度$\widetilde O(L)$，需$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$个辅助比特。该构造绕过了精确块编码中辅助比特开销的下界限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准块编码构造通常需要对数级别的辅助比特开销和复杂的受控操作，且近期下界证明在精确构造中这种辅助比特开销在广泛电路模型下不可避免。探索是否可以在近似设置中绕过这一障碍。

Method: 基于广义量子信号处理，提出一种单辅助比特构造，将哈密顿量演化转化为底层哈密顿量的块编码。对于厄米分解$A=\sum_{j=1}^L α_j H_j$，分别采用高阶Trotterization（仅需1个辅助比特）和多乘积公式（需$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$个辅助比特）两种方式实例化所需的哈密顿量演化。

Result: 高阶Trotterization方案：$\varepsilon$-近似块编码，1个辅助比特，电路深度$\widetilde O(L(\alpha/\varepsilon)^{o(1)})$；多乘积公式方案：电路深度$\widetilde O(L)$，辅助比特$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$。两种方案均为标准LCU框架提供了替代方案。

Conclusion: 证明了近似块编码可以绕过精确构造的辅助比特开销下界。所提构造在保持（近）最优电路深度的同时显著减少了辅助比特数量。

Abstract: Block encodings are a central primitive in quantum algorithms, but standard constructions typically require logarithmic ancilla overhead and complicated controlled operations. Recent lower bounds further show that such ancilla overhead is unavoidable for exact constructions in broad circuit models. We show that this barrier can be bypassed in the approximate setting. Specifically, we present a simple single-ancilla construction that converts Hamiltonian evolution into a block encoding of the underlying Hamiltonian, via generalized quantum signal processing. For operators given by Hermitian decompositions $A=\sum_{j=1}^L α_j H_j$, we instantiate this block-encoding construction in two ways, which differ in how the required Hamiltonian evolution is implemented. Using higher-order Trotterization, we obtain an $\varepsilon$-approximate block encoding of $A$ with only one ancilla qubit and circuit depth $\widetilde O\big(L(α/\varepsilon)^{o(1)}\big),$ where $α=\sum_j α_j$. Using multiproduct formulas, we obtain circuit depth $\widetilde O(L)$, at the cost of $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ ancilla qubits. Our constructions provide alternatives to the standard LCU framework, with a focus on reducing the number of ancilla qubits while maintaining (near-)optimal circuit depth.

</details>


### [134] [LUCI on IBM Hardware: Error Suppression with Almost Half Syndrome Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01887)
*Younghun Kim,Spiro Gicev,Martin Sevior,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM量子硬件上实验验证了LUCI框架的无重置方案，通过非对称缩放X或Z距离，展示了动态码在无物理缺陷情况下仍能通过规避高噪声组件超越标准表面码的纠错性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠错协议依赖特定综合征电路，易受硬件缺陷影响且对连接拓扑有刚性约束。LUCI框架虽可维持时空距离，但此前仅针对缺陷规避，未在物理硬件上验证时间距离缩减下的纠错效果。

Method: 提出LUCI框架的无重置场景，在IBM量子硬件上进行实验基准测试。通过非对称缩放X或Z距离，将无重置方法与标准表面码对比，测量目标逻辑Pauli错误的抑制比。

Result: LUCI框架中逻辑X错误抑制比为1.75(10)，逻辑Z错误抑制比为1.93(12)；标准表面码分别为1.58(13)和2.44(7)。尽管LUCI的综合征密度减半（需两个子程序轮次完成全综合征提取），其性能仍与旋转表面码具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 动态码即使无物理缺陷也能通过规避高噪声组件超越静态容错架构，验证了LUCI框架在真实硬件上的可行性与有效性，为混合硬件兼容的量子计算码设计开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Long-lived logical qubits are essential for fault-tolerant quantum computation. However, the practical performance of traditional error correction protocols relies on performing specific syndrome circuits, causing vulnerability to hardware defects and imposing rigid connectivity constraints. Recent theoretical findings have proposed that flexible subroutine circuits within the LUCI framework can maintain space-time distance in the presence of isolated or broken components, albeit at the expense of temporal distance. However, these approaches have solely targeted defect avoidance and have not yet been demonstrated to suppress errors with reduced temporal distances on physical hardware. In this work, we propose a reset-free scenario for the LUCI framework and experimentally benchmark it on IBM quantum hardware. By asymmetrically scaling the $X$ or $Z$ distance, we compare our reset-free approach against the standard surface code and successfully demonstrate error suppression ratios for targeted logical Pauli errors. Remarkably, despite a nearly halved syndrome density in time, which requires two subroutine rounds for full syndrome extraction, the LUCI framework remains competitive with the rotated surface code implementation. In the LUCI framework, we observe error suppression of $1.75(10)$ for logical $X$ errors and $1.93(12)$ for logical $Z$ errors, whereas the standard approach yields $ 1.58(13)$ and $2.44(7)$, respectively. These results demonstrate that dynamic codes outperform standard methods by avoiding highly noisy components, even without physical defects, while preserving logical boundaries. Our findings challenge the conventional dependency on static fault-tolerant architectures by verifying the feasibility and efficacy of the LUCI framework on physical hardware and pave the way for hybrid, hardware-compatible code designs in quantum computing.

</details>


### [135] [Growth of Schrödinger cats in particle-number measurement schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01911)
*S. B. Korolev,A. A. Silin,A. A. Poshevkina,T. Yu. Golubeva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于光子数分辨测量的多模高斯态产生压缩薛定谔猫态的方案，推导了二模和三模方案的解析表达式及保真度公式，分析了生成态振幅与探测粒子数的关系，并给出了保真度上界及多模方案提升生成态质量的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用多模高斯态和光子数分辨测量来有效生成压缩薛定谔猫态，并理解其在不同参数下的生成质量与保真度极限。

Method: 通过解析推导二模和三模高斯态经光子数分辨测量后的条件态表达式，计算其与目标压缩薛定谔猫态的保真度，并分析振幅缩放关系及保真度上界。

Result: 得到了二模和三模方案下生成的量子态解析形式、保真度公式、振幅与探测粒子数的缩放关系，以及保真度的上界表达式。

Conclusion: 多模方案能在特定条件下提升生成态质量，并且存在保真度上界，为实验实现高质量压缩薛定谔猫态提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the generation of squeezed Schrödinger cat states in schemes based on photon-number-resolving measurements on multimode Gaussian states. We derive analytical expressions for the states generated in two- and three-mode schemes, as well as formulas for their fidelity with squeezed Schrödinger cat states. We analyze how the amplitude of the generated states scales with the number of detected particles. Furthermore, we derive an upper bound on the achievable generation fidelity and identify the conditions under which multimode schemes can enhance the quality of the generated states.

</details>


### [136] [A Quantum-Walk Representation of Color-Ordered MHV Scattering Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02456)
*Anirudh Verma,C. M. Chandrashekar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图论的框架，通过置换树上的量子游走来表示QCD中颜色排序的MHV散射振幅，建立了量子游走、置换组合学与量子场论之间的联系，并给出了低点胶子振幅的数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在量子计算框架中模拟QCD散射过程，需要一种能够自然编码颜色排序组合结构的表示方法，将量子场论的振幅计算与量子算法的实现联系起来。

Method: 利用置换树上的量子游走表示颜色排序，每个根到终端路径对应一种颜色排序；根据Parke-Taylor振幅的自旋积结构分配局部转移振幅；通过Kraus算子的量子信道公式描述扇区分辨贡献；使用加权收集算子在公共参考节点相干组合终端扇区；通过硬币空间上的量子傅里叶变换组合编码贡献得到颜色分解振幅。

Result: 低点胶子振幅的数值结果表明，该表示能够忠实捕捉Parke-Taylor结构的特征，并与解析结果一致。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了置换树、量子游走和开放量子系统，为量子算法模拟量子场论中的散射过程提供了可行的理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a graph-theoretic framework for representing color-ordered maximally helicity violating (MHV) scattering amplitudes in quantum chromodynamics using coined quantum walks on permutation trees. Each root-to-terminal path corresponds to a distinct color ordering of the external gluons, while local transition amplitudes are assigned according to the spinor-product structure of the Parke--Taylor amplitudes. The walk evolves in coherent superpositions over permutation sectors, giving a dynamical picture of the underlying combinatorics. A quantum-channel formulation based on Kraus operators is also introduced to describe sector-resolved contributions, while a weighted collection operator coherently combines the terminal sectors at a common reference node. A quantum Fourier transform on the coin space is then employed to combine the encoded contributions into the corresponding color-decomposed amplitude. Together, these constructions establish a unified graph-based framework connecting permutation trees, quantum walks, and open quantum systems providing a framework for quantum algorithms to simulate scattering processes in quantum field theory. As an example, numerical results for low-point gluon amplitudes demonstrate that the proposed representation faithfully captures the characteristic Parke--Taylor structure and is consistent with analytical results.

</details>


### [137] [Hacking measurement-device-independent quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01989)
*Konstantin Zaitsev,Polina Acheva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了测量设备无关量子密钥分发（MDI-QKD）在标准安全模型下的一个根本性局限：当测量节点（Charlie）被对手完全控制时，攻击者可通过主动操控测量设备获取高达70%的筛选密钥，同时仅引入5.6%的量子误码率，这表明现有安全假设不足以应对现实中的强敌手场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDI-QKD旨在通过将测量任务交给完全不可信方来消除单光子探测器的安全漏洞，但该协议隐含假设测量节点虽可被敌手控制却不影响安全性。本文的动机是检验这一假设在现实对抗条件下是否成立。

Method: 作者提出了一种针对MDI-QKD协议的主动攻击策略，攻击者完全控制测量节点Charlie，通过操控测量过程获取密钥信息，并分析攻击在量子误码率（QBER）和密钥信息泄露量两个维度上的效果。

Result: 攻击可在仅引入5.6% QBER的条件下恢复高达70%的筛选密钥，表明标准安全模型在实际中是不充分的。

Conclusion: 现有MDI-QKD的测量设备独立性假设需要额外的约束条件，或者需采用考虑更强敌手能力的精细化安全分析，以保证协议在现实场景中的安全性。

Abstract: The security of practical quantum key distribution (QKD) systems is fundamentally constrained by vulnerabilities of single-photon detectors. Measurement-device-independent quantum key distribution (MDI-QKD) was proposed to remove this limitation by allowing all measurements to be performed by a completely untrusted party, under the assumption that the measurement node can be treated as adversarial but does not compromise the security guarantees of the protocol.
  Here we show that this assumption is insufficient under realistic adversarial control of the measurement device. We present an attack in which an adversary exploits active control of the measurement node (Charlie) to obtain significant information about the secret key. The attack enables recovery of up to 70\% of the sifted key while introducing only 5.6\% quantum bit error rate. Unlike previously reported attacks targeting specific implementations of MDI-QKD, our results demonstrate a limitation of the standard security model underlying the protocol.
  These findings indicate that additional constraints on the measurement-device independence assumption, or refined security analyses incorporating stronger adversarial capabilities, are required to ensure the security of MDI-QKD in realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [138] [Topological Control of Quantum Chaos Diagnostics: OTOCs, Spectral Statistics, and Information Scrambling in Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02463)
*Reza Pirmoradian,Soheir Rouhani,M. Reza Tanhayi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过图论形式化方法研究伊辛自旋网络中的可积-混沌转变与信息置乱，在路径图、Erdős–Rényi图和Watts–Strogatz图拓扑上，证明长程耦合与异质性度分布会显著加速量子信息传播。通过调节非局域耦合与场异质性驱动可积性破缺，利用二分互信息和三分信息量化置乱，结合OTOC、Krylov复杂度、能级统计和谱形因子建立统一分析框架，揭示拓扑结构如何影响信息、算符和谱学诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立连接网络拓扑结构与量子信息置乱、算符动力学和谱学诊断指标的统一理论框架，深入理解量子多体系统中的热化与非平衡动力学。

Method: 采用图论形式化方法，将自旋建模为顶点，通过邻接矩阵表示相互作用；构造包含局域和归一化非局域相互作用的哈密顿量；使用二分互信息、三分信息、OTOC、Krylov复杂度、能级统计（Poissonian到Wigner-Dyson转变）和谱形因子（SFF）等多种诊断工具。

Result: 长程耦合和异质性度分布加速量子信息传播；增加非局域相互作用使三分信息大幅负值化，标志深度信息置乱；OTOC指数早期增长给出量子Lyapunov指数；Krylov复杂度在混沌相中快速增长；能级统计从Poissonian转变为Wigner-Dyson；SFF呈现斜率-下降-斜坡-平台结构；减小的Thouless时间与加速的信息和算符置乱强相关。

Conclusion: 建立了一个统一框架，将网络拓扑与信息论、算符和谱学诊断指标联系起来，为量子多体系统的热化和非平衡动力学提供了深刻见解。

Abstract: We investigate the integrability-to-chaos transition and information scrambling in Ising spin networks via a graph-theoretic formulation. Modeling spins as vertices and interactions via adjacency matrices across path, Erdős--Rényi, and Watts--Strogatz topologies, we demonstrate that long-range couplings and heterogeneous degree distributions drastically accelerate quantum information propagation. The Hamiltonian comprises local and normalized non-local interactions; tuning the non-local coupling and field heterogeneity drives integrability breaking. To quantify scrambling, we employ bipartite mutual and tripartite information. Increasing non-local interactions drives tripartite information to large negative values, signaling deep information scrambling. Out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) exhibit exponential early-time growth, yielding quantum Lyapunov exponents that scale systematically with parameters governing the chaotic regime. Complementing this, Krylov complexity reveals rapid operator growth in the chaotic phase, synchronizing with OTOC and mutual information dynamics. Spectrally, the transition manifests as a shift from Poissonian to Wigner--Dyson level spacing statistics. The spectral form factor (SFF) exhibits the characteristic slope-dip-ramp-plateau structure, enabling the extraction of Thouless and Heisenberg times. Crucially, a reduced Thouless time strongly correlates with accelerated informational and operator scrambling. Ultimately, this work establishes a unified framework bridging network topology with information-theoretic, operator, and spectral diagnostics, offering profound insights into thermalization and non-equilibrium dynamics in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [139] [Partially-Blind Single-Qubit Classification over a Prototype Hybrid Quantum Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01998)
*Matteo Pasini,Tzula Benjamin Propp,Janice van Dam,Garazi Muguruza Lasa,Alexandre Wanick,Hugues de Riedmatten,Gustavo C. do Amaral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分盲单量子比特分类器（PB-SQC）框架，可在NISQ时代实现资源高效的量子机器学习，通过量子网络为远程客户端提供具有信息论安全性的数据分类服务，并在模拟中展示了与经典深度置信网络相当的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NISQ时代，需要资源高效的原理验证实验，并能逐步扩展到实际应用。同时，需要将盲量子计算（BQC）的安全优势与量子机器学习相结合，实现数据隐私保护的分类任务。

Method: 提出了部分盲单量子比特分类器（PB-SQC）框架，服务器知道正在进行分类任务，但数据和结果保持隐藏。利用异构量子网络链路，通过纠缠交换在服务器和配备多路复用量子存储器的客户端之间共享纠缠，并结合经典深度置信网络进行仿真验证。

Result: 在真实信用卡交易欺诈数据库上的模拟显示，PB-SQC的分类结果接近等效经典深度置信网络。同时展示了双量子比特分类器（TQC）可实现对计算的验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为短期内构建提供实用量子应用的量子网络铺平了道路，将量子机器学习的安全性与量子通信的隐私保护能力相结合。

Abstract: In the NISQ era, there is a need for resource-efficient proof-of-principle experiments that can be built up to genuine utility. Single-qubit classifiers (SQCs) are small-scale hybrid quantum-classical machines capable of performing a basic machine learning task: classifying data. In principle, these can be scaled up to many-qubit quantum classifiers capable of quantum computational advantage. Another type of quantum advantage is enabled by blind quantum computation (BQC), wherein a client may run delegated quantum computations on an untrusted server with information-theoretic security. In this paper, we develop a framework and propose a prototype experiment for a SQC where it is known to the server that a classification is being performed, but the data and outcome stay hidden, i.e., it performs partially-blind SQC (PB-SQC). This can be integrated into a quantum network to deliver quantum-secured classifications to remote clients; we study this for a heterogeneous quantum network link in which entanglement is shared between a server and a client equipped with a multiplexed solid-state quantum memory using entanglement swapping. The framework we develop for PB-SQC on this setup is tested in a simulation with realistic hardware parameters on a real-world credit card transaction fraud database with classification outcomes approaching those of its equivalent classical deep-belief network. In addition, we show how a two-qubit classifier (TQC) instead of a SQC enables verification of the computation. These results pave the way towards a short- to mid-term quantum network offering use-case-ready quantum applications.

</details>


### [140] [Compressive Spectrum Sensing via Spectral Multiplexing in Rydberg Atomic Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02001)
*Jun-Rong Chen,Yi-Ming Yin,Le-Bin Chen,Kai Wang,Bang Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Hao Tian,Ming-Min Zhao,Bin-Bin Wei,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于压缩频谱复用的波导耦合里德伯原子接收机架构，通过调频本地振荡器（FMLO）将宽带微波频谱压缩到原子固有的窄带响应中，实现了超过1000倍的频谱压缩比（640 MHz→126 kHz），并利用输出副本的测量冗余实现了约10 dB的信噪比增益，无需辅助场或宽带电子设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里德伯原子接收机虽具有极高灵敏度，但其固有瞬时带宽极窄，难以满足宽带应用场景需求。现有通过物理展宽原子响应的方法需要辅助电磁场或严格参数调谐，增加了系统复杂度。

Method: 提出压缩频谱复用框架：使用调频本地振荡器（FMLO）在波导耦合里德伯原子接收机中创建多个并行感知通道，构成物理压缩感知矩阵，将宽带微波频谱投影到多个窄带中频副本上，实现频谱压缩。

Result: 实现了640 MHz以上带宽压缩至126 kHz原子固有带宽（压缩比>1000）；通过最大比合并（MRC）在多信道通信中获得约10 dB的每比特能量与噪声功率密度比（Eb/N0）增益。

Conclusion: 该方法无需辅助场或宽带电子器件，为芯片级量子接收机、低延迟感知和下一代无线通信提供了简单、可扩展的实现路径。

Abstract: Rydberg-atomic receivers exhibit exceptional sensitivity yet are fundamentally constrained by the narrow instantaneous bandwidth, limiting their practical deployment in broadband scenarios. Prior approaches typically expand the bandwidth by physically broadening the atomic response, which usually requires auxiliary electromagnetic fields or stringent parameter tuning, thereby increasing overall system complexity. Here, we propose a compressive spectral multiplexing framework implemented in a waveguide-coupled Rydberg atomic receiver using a frequency-modulated local oscillator (FMLO). The FMLO creates multiple parallel sensing channels that collectively constitute a physical compressive sensing matrix, generating multiple narrowband intermediate-frequency replicas of the input signal. Thus, a broadband microwave spectrum is projected onto a set of narrowband atomic responses. It is demonstrated that spectral information spanning a bandwidth of over 640 MHz can be effectively compressed into the intrinsic atomic bandwidth of 126 kHz, achieving a spectrum compression ratio exceeding 1000. Furthermore, these output replicas offer intrinsic measurement redundancy and facilitate signal-to-noise ratio enhancement. An approximate 10 dB gain is achieved in the required bit-energy-to-noise-power-density ratio for multi-channel communication via maximal-ratio combining. This approach requires no auxiliary fields or broadband electronics, providing a simple and scalable pathway for chip-scale quantum receivers, latency-critical sensing, and next-generation wireless communications.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum sensing of aging transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02004)
*Huining Zhang,Yunbo Zhang,Xiaoguang Wang,X. X. Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用单量子比特探针来检测量子振荡器网络中老化相变点的方法，通过Fisher信息的临界增强实现高精度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老化相变是量子网络中集体动力学退化的关键现象，检测其相变点对于预测网络崩溃、稳定性评估和鲁棒性控制至关重要。

Method: 使用单个量子比特探针与一小部分振荡器节点相干耦合，通过监测探针激发态布居数对非活跃节点分数p的敏感性，利用Fisher信息的临界增强来定位老化相变点。

Result: 当非活跃分数p趋近老化相变点时，探针激发态布居数对p的变化高度敏感，Fisher信息显著增强；即使在经典振荡器区域，这种增强依然存在。

Conclusion: 该研究为振荡器网络中的老化相变检测提供了可行途径，并从计量学视角为量子多体系统中的临界现象提供了新理解。

Abstract: The aging transition is a critical phenomenon in which collective dynamics deteriorate as the fraction of inactive quantum nodes exceeds a threshold, referred to as the aging transition point. Such transitions are relevant to a broad range of biological and physiological systems, and may play an important role in quantum information processing, particularly in the stability assessment and robustness control of quantum networks. Detecting the aging transition point is therefore crucial for predicting network breakdown, since it marks the critical threshold at which a quantum network abruptly loses its stable active state and enters a degraded inactive phase. Here we propose a quantum sensing strategy to locate this transition point using a single qubit probe coherently coupled to a small subset of oscillator nodes. As the inactive fraction p approaches the aging transition point, the excited-state population of the probe becomes highly sensitive to variations in p, leading to a pronounced enhancement of the Fisher information. This critical enhancement enables high-precision estimation of the transition point. Remarkably, this enhancement survives even in the classical regime for the oscillators, where the Fisher information increases dramatically as p approaches the transition region. Our results establish a feasible route to sensing aging transitions in oscillator networks and provide a metrological perspective on critical phenomena in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [142] [Local distinguishability of six bipartite orthogonal product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02006)
*Guang-Bao Xu,Hua-Kun Wang,Yu-Guang Yang,Dong-Huan Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二分量子系统中六个正交乘积态(OPSs)的局域可区分性，基于正交关系数向量将其分为8类共78种不同情况，发现其中73种可通过LOCC完美区分，并对剩余5种情况进行了详细讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交量子态集合的局域可区分性研究有助于减少协议设计中的量子态传输和操作成本，因此有必要对其进行深入探索。

Method: 利用两两正交关系数的向量将六个二分正交乘积态集合分为8个类别；识别出共78种不同情况；通过LOCC分析各类别的可区分性。

Result: 78种不同情况中，除5种外其余73种均可通过LOCC完美区分。

Conclusion: 本文明确刻画了六个二分正交乘积态集合的局域可区分性特征。

Abstract: It is necessary to investigate the local distinguishability of orthogonal quantum state sets, as their adoption in protocol design helps diminish quantum state transmission and cut operational costs. In this paper, we explore the local distinguishability of six orthogonal product states (OPSs) on any bipartite quantum system. We classify different sets of six bipartite OPSs into eight categories by using the vectors of the numbers of pairwise orthogonality relations, where any two states are orthogonal on only one subsystem within each set. We find that these eight categories contain a total of 78 distinct cases, all but five of which are perfectly distinguishable via local operations and classical communication (LOCC). Furthermore, we discuss the local distinguishability of those five distinct cases in detail. Our work explicitly characterizes the local distinguishability of six bipartite OPSs.

</details>


### [143] [Mid-infrared pure-state quantum light source based on lithium niobate waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02016)
*Huang Yuhang,Wang Dongzhou,Ke Shaolin,Jin Ruibo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于铌酸锂薄膜的中红外量子光源理论方案，通过1556.9 nm泵浦产生中心波长为3113.8 nm的纠缠光子对。优化波导结构和周期极化设计实现II类相位匹配和群速度匹配，结合域排列算法实现精确相位匹配，获得了纯度高达0.999、亮度为6.18×10^6 cps/mW的量子光源，亮度比体PPLN晶体源高三个数量级，为实现中红外波段高亮度、高纯度的片上量子光源提供了有前景的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中红外量子纠缠光源主要依赖体PPLN晶体，在亮度和集成度方面存在局限，亟需发展高亮度、高纯度的片上集成中红外量子光源。

Method: 基于铌酸锂薄膜，采用1556.9 nm泵浦，通过优化波导结构和周期极化设计实现II类相位匹配和群速度匹配，结合用于PPLN波导偏振方向定制设计的域排列算法，实现TE偏振泵浦输入下产生TE和TM偏振的光子对的下转换过程。

Result: 实现了纯度高达0.999、亮度为6.18×10^6 cps/mW的量子光源，其亮度比体PPLN晶体光源高出三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该研究为实现中红外波段高亮度、高纯度的片上集成量子光源提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Mid-infrared quantum light sources hold broad application prospects in fields such as gas sensing and infrared thermal imaging. However, currently used mid-infrared quantum entangled light sources primarily rely on bulk periodically poled lithium niobate (PPLN) crystals, which limits brightness and integration. This paper proposes a theoretical scheme based on lithium niobate thin films, in which 1556.9 nm pumping is used to generate entangled photon pairs with a central wavelength of 3113.8 nm. By optimizing the waveguide structure and periodic polarization design, type-II phase matching and group velocity matching are achieved. This enables transverse electric (TE)-polarized pump input to be down converted to generate photon pairs with TE and transverse magnetic (TM) polarizations. Furthermore, by combining a domain arrangement algorithm used for the customized design of polarization direction in PPLN waveguides, precise phase matching is achieved, resulting in a quantum light source with a purity as high as 0.999 and a brightness of 6.18$\times 10^6$ cps/mW, which is three orders of magnitude higher than that of the bulk PPLN crystal source. This study provides a promising solution for realizing high-brightness, high-purity on-chip quantum light sources in the mid-infrared band.

</details>


### [144] [Idling error suppression through gate scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02031)
*Hoiki Madison Liu,Kazunori Maruyama,Hirotaka Oshima,Shintaro Sato*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过量子电路调度（调整门执行时序）来抑制空闲错误，无需引入额外门操作，并通过数值模拟和硬件实验验证了该方法能显著影响并提升计算精度，同时提供了理论分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度量子计算需要有效抑制空闲错误（qubit在等待期间不活跃时发生的错误），传统方法如动态解耦需要插入额外控制门，增加了电路复杂度和资源开销。

Method: 提出通过量子电路调度（调整具有调度灵活性的量子门执行时序）来抑制空闲错误，不引入任何额外的门操作。通过数值模拟和硬件实验验证，并解析推导了空闲噪声下的密度矩阵演化作为理论框架。

Result: 数值模拟和硬件实验均表明，适当调整量子门的执行时序可以显著影响整体计算精度，并且在许多情况下能够提升精度。

Conclusion: 量子电路调度是一种无需额外门操作即可有效抑制空闲错误的新方法，可替代或补充传统的动态解耦技术，为量子计算错误缓解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Achieving high-precision quantum computation requires effective suppression of idling errors that occur when qubits remain inactive during waiting periods within a quantum circuit. Conventional mitigation techniques, such as dynamical decoupling, suppress decoherence by periodically refreshing quantum states through the insertion of additional control gates. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach that suppresses idling errors through quantum circuit scheduling without introducing any additional gate operations. By appropriately adjusting the execution timing of quantum gates with scheduling flexibility, we demonstrate through both numerical simulations and hardware experiments that the overall computational accuracy can be significantly influenced and, in many cases, improved. In addition, we analytically derive the density-matrix evolution under idling noise and provide a theoretical framework that explains the observed behavior.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum Convolutional Autoencoders for Reconstruction-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02135)
*Donovan Slabbert,Francesco Petruccione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子卷积神经网络(QCNN)改编为量子自编码器(QAE)框架，用于基于重构的异常检测。研究了两种量子卷积自编码器架构——层次化架构和瓶颈架构，在真实系外行星异常检测数据集上对比了变分量子电路和经典基线方法。结果表明，通过量子瓶颈进行显式潜在空间压缩能提升异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCNNs因其高效参数化和量子信息的层次化表示在量子机器学习中日益流行。异常检测是重要的机器学习任务，应用广泛。本文旨在将QCNN架构适配到量子自编码器框架中，用于基于重构的异常检测，探索量子模型在该任务中的有效性。

Method: 将QCNN架构改编为量子自编码器(QAE)，以半监督方式在正常样本上训练，重构特征提取和降维后的时间序列数据，使用重构误差作为异常分数。研究了两种量子卷积自编码器架构：(1)层次化架构——信息在电路中保持分布式；(2)瓶颈架构——使用额外解码器量子比特显式压缩和重构信息。变化量子潜在空间大小以研究其对重构精度和异常检测性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明潜在空间大小与模型容量之间存在权衡，同时表明通过量子瓶颈进行显式潜在空间压缩相比在电路中保留信息的架构能提升异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 量子瓶颈架构在异常检测任务上优于层次化架构，说明显式压缩对于量子异常检测更有效；潜在空间大小与模型容量存在权衡关系。

Abstract: Quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) have become increasingly popular in quantum machine learning (QML) due to their efficient parameterization and hierarchical representation of quantum information. Anomaly detection is an important machine learning task with applications across a wide range of domains, including scientific data analysis. In this work, we adapt a QCNN architecture into a quantum autoencoder (QAE) framework for reconstruction-based anomaly detection. The models are trained in a semi-supervised manner on normal samples to reconstruct feature-extracted and dimensionally reduced time-series data, with reconstruction error used as an anomaly score. We investigate two quantum convolutional autoencoder architectures that differ in their treatment of latent information: a hierarchical architecture in which information remains distributed across the circuit and a bottleneck-based architecture in which information is explicitly compressed and reconstructed using additional decoder qubits. The size of the quantum latent space is varied to study its influence on reconstruction accuracy and anomaly detection performance. The approaches are benchmarked against both a variational quantum circuit and a comparable classical baseline using a real-world exoplanet anomaly-detection dataset. Results indicate a trade-off between latent-space size and model capacity, while also suggesting that explicit latent-space compression through a quantum bottleneck can improve anomaly detection performance relative to architectures that retain information throughout the circuit.

</details>


### [146] [Extending the computational reach of Quantum Annealing using Reverse Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02146)
*Lucas Joshua Menger,Thomas Lippert,Manpreet Singh Jattana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验证明，反向退火在组合优化中能够持续提升解质量和效率，尤其适用于大规模高复杂度问题，且其优势随问题复杂度增加而增强，效果优于单纯延长正向退火时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子退火硬件在处理大规模复杂问题时，因噪声和小能隙导致性能退化，而反向退火虽被提出作为改进策略，但其何时能提供系统性优势仍不明确，因此需要系统性研究其适用条件与最优参数。

Method: 在D-Wave Advantage系统上，针对Max-Cut、Number Partitioning和稀疏聚类三类问题进行系统性实验，通过改变反向距离、暂停时长和退火时间等参数，对比正向退火与反向退火的性能差异。

Result: 反向退火联合正向退火能持续提升解质量和效率；其优势随问题复杂度增加而增强；在正向退火受限的区间效果最显著；通过延长正向退火时间无法获得同等效率提升；存在一个与冻结点和能级交叉位置相关的窄最优参数区间。

Conclusion: 反向退火对大规模高复杂度优化问题最有价值，随着量子退火硬件向更实际应用扩展，其重要性将进一步增加。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising heuristic for combinatorial optimization, but on current hardware its performance degrades for larger and more complex problems due to noise and small energy gaps. Reverse annealing has been proposed as a refinement strategy, yet it remains unclear when it provides systematic advantages over standard forward annealing or simply increasing annealing time. We find that combining forward and reverse annealing consistently improves solution quality and efficiency across multiple problem classes. The benefits of reverse annealing increase with problem complexity and are strongest in regimes where forward annealing is increasingly limited. Moreover, reverse annealing yields larger efficiency gains than simply extending forward annealing times. We establish these results through a systematic experimental study on a D-Wave Advantage system, benchmarking reverse annealing across Max-Cut, Number Partitioning, and sparse clustering problems while varying reverse distance, pause duration, and annealing time. We identify a narrow optimal regime for reverse annealing parameters linked to the location of freeze-out points and energy-level crossings in the annealing schedule. These findings demonstrate that reverse annealing is most valuable for large, high-complexity optimization problems and is likely to gain importance as quantum annealing hardware scales toward more realistic applications.

</details>


### [147] [A Structure Theorem for Phase-Space Representations of Continuous-Variable Quantum Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02164)
*Enrico Bozzetto,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义概率论中准概率表示的结构定理与玻色量子纠错码联系起来，为连续变量纠错码提供了通用的相空间表示，并具体展示了GKP码、猫码和二项式码的相空间表示。这种表示能够定义错误在相空间中可能采取的数学结构，文中对单光子丢失错误进行了抽象和具体分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立广义概率论中准概率表示的结构定理与玻色量子纠错码之间的理论联系，为连续变量量子纠错码提供统一的相空间描述框架。

Method: 通过连接准概率表示的结构定理与玻色量子纠错码，推导出通用的相空间表示方法，并以GKP码、猫码和二项式码作为具体案例进行验证。

Result: 成功为连续变量纠错码定义了通用的相空间表示，并具体展示了GKP码、猫码和二项式码的相空间结构，同时定义了错误在相空间中的数学结构。

Conclusion: 该相空间表示方法能够统一描述多种玻色量子纠错码，并有效刻画错误（如单光子丢失）的相空间结构。

Abstract: In this paper we connect the structure theorem for quasiprobability representation of generalised probabilistic theories to bosonic quantum error correction codes, giving both a general phase-space representation for continuous-variable error-correcting codes, and showing as specific examples the phase-space representations obtained through this method for Gottesman-Knill-Preskill codes, cat codes, and binomial codes. This representation allows us to define both generally and for each of these codes the mathematical structure in phase space that errors can take, which we show both abstractly and for the specific example of single photon loss errors.

</details>


### [148] [Neural-Network Inverse Design of SRF Cavities and Transmons for Bosonic Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02289)
*Joseph Yaker,Jovan Markovic,Alessandro Reineri,Doga Murat Kurkcuoglu,Silvia Zorzetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种深度神经网络方法，分别用于超导射频腔的几何逆设计和transmon量子比特的设计，能够直接从目标性能参数（腔体特性或耦合率、量子比特频率、非谐性）映射到候选设计，精度分别达到~5%和~2%，替代了传统迭代仿真流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维SRF腔与transmon量子比特耦合的量子信息处理系统中，器件几何结构与电磁性能之间存在一对多的逆设计问题。随着系统规模扩大，传统迭代仿真方法的计算成本变得难以承受。

Method: 构建了两个互补级别的深度神经网络：第一个网络用于SRF腔体几何的逆设计，从目标腔体可观测量生成腔体结构；第二个网络用于transmon量子比特的设计，从目标参数（耦合率g、量子比特频率ν_q、非谐性α）生成transmon设计。

Result: 恢复的候选设计经端到端重仿真验证，腔体候选设计与目标偏差约5%，transmon候选设计与目标偏差约2%。

Conclusion: 该方法为量子器件逆设计提供了快速替代方案，直接从期望行为映射到候选设计，避免了传统迭代仿真的高昂计算开销，适用于扩展中的量子信息处理系统。

Abstract: Three-dimensional superconducting radio-frequency (SRF) cavities provide exceptionally long-lived electromagnetic modes and, when coupled to nonlinear elements such as transmon qubits, become promising architectures for bosonic quantum information processing. The inverse design of such systems, i.e., recovering device geometries that produce specified electromagnetic and coupling targets, is generally a one-to-many problem. The qubit-cavity coupling strength depends sensitively on both the transmon geometry and its position within the cavity's electromagnetic field. As these systems scale up and their design parameter spaces grow, the cost of conventional iterative simulation becomes prohibitive. We present two deep neural network (DNN) approaches that address this inverse-design problem at complementary levels of the design stack. The first proposes SRF cavity geometries that produce target cavity observables. The second proposes transmon qubit designs that produce target qubit-cavity parameters -- the coupling rate, qubit frequency, and anharmonicity $(g, ν_q, α)$. The recovered candidate designs match the targets to within $\sim$5\% (cavity) and $\sim$2\% (transmon), confirmed by end-to-end re-simulation. Both approaches map desired device behavior directly to candidate designs, a fast alternative to the iterative simulation studies usually required.

</details>


### [149] [Time-Reversal and Reversible Dynamics in Cavity QED for Quantum Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02320)
*Simone Colombo,Edwin Pedrozo-Peñafiel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了腔量子电动力学中基于时间反演协议的量子增强测量方法，核心思想是利用可逆多体动力学既产生纠缠又解码信息，从而突破标准量子极限。重点讨论了SATIN协议、 scrambling增强计量学等方案，指出解码能力将与纠缠生成能力同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强计量学虽能利用纠缠实现超越标准量子极限的灵敏度，但编码在纠缠态中的信息往往难以通过实际测量提取，因此需要发展有效的解码策略。

Method: 利用可逆的多体相互作用实现时间反演协议：先通过正向相互作用产生纠缠并编码信号，再通过反向（时间反演）相互作用将微弱信号放大为实验可观测的物理量。具体方案包括SATIN（时间反演相互作用的信号放大）、scrambling增强计量学以及更一般的基于相互作用的读出方案。

Result: 腔QED平台因其集体增强、可调相互作用和可控可逆性，成为实现时间反演协议的理想平台。实验已在多种系统中展示了可逆多体动力学在量子测量中的应用。

Conclusion: 解码量子信息的能力将与生成纠缠的能力同等重要，可逆多体动力学应被确立为量子增强传感的核心资源，未来方向包括复杂纠缠态、非线性解码及新兴量子平台。

Abstract: Quantum-enhanced metrology relies on entanglement to achieve sensitivities beyond the standard quantum limit. While remarkable progress has been made in generating highly entangled many-body states, extracting their metrological advantage remains a central challenge because the encoded information is often inaccessible to realistic measurements. A key development of the past decade has been the realization that many-body interactions can play a dual role: they can be used not only to generate entanglement, but also to decode it. This idea underlies interaction-based readout and time-reversal protocols, in which controlled non-linear dynamics transform weakly encoded signals into experimentally accessible observables. Cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) provides a particularly powerful setting for these approaches because it combines collective enhancement, tunable interactions, and controllable reversibility within a single platform. In this review, we discuss the emergence of time-reversal protocols in cavity QED, from their conceptual roots in Loschmidt echoes to modern implementations of signal amplification through a time-reversed interaction (SATIN), scrambling-enhanced metrology, and more general interaction-based readout schemes. We examine the physical mechanisms that enable reversible many-body dynamics, review key experimental demonstrations, and discuss future directions involving complex entangled states, nonlinear decoding, and emerging quantum platforms. Together, these developments suggest that the ability to decode quantum information may become as important as the ability to generate it, establishing reversible many-body dynamics as a central resource for quantum-enhanced sensing.

</details>


### [150] [Temporal nonlocality of a qudit resides in the input state, not the channel, and certifies temporal teleportation up to a fundamental limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02331)
*Karol Bartkiewicz,Patrycja Tulewicz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间非局域鲁棒性（TNR），发现单量子系统通过噪声通道时，TNR完全由初始状态承载：输入为最大混合态时TNR为零。TNR可作为时间隐形传态的保真度下界，但存在过度认证问题——即TNR可能认证了通道实际未传递的量子资源。通过引入半定规划层次结构统一了时间纠缠、导引和非局域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究单个量子系统在两次测量间的时间关联（时间非局域性）的本质，澄清这种关联是由初始状态还是通道承载，并解决时间非局域性鲁棒性（TNR）作为资源认证工具时的可靠性问题。

Method: 理论推导TNR与初始状态混合度的关系；构建半定规划（SDP）层次结构统一时间纠缠（TER）、时间导引（TSR）和时间非局域鲁棒性（TNR）；数值验证d=2至5的情况。

Result: TNR≠0当且仅当输入非完全混合态；TNR设备无关地给出时间隐形传态保真度的下界（d=3时达7/9）；证明了TNR存在通用上限(d-1)/d；发现可逆酉通道可实现最大TNR但隐形传态低于经典基线，即过度认证问题。

Conclusion: 时间非局域性主要由初始状态的非混合度承载而非通道相干性；TNR作为资源度量存在局限，需结合具体通道和探针态解释；提出的SDP层次结构为时间量子关联提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Correlations between two moments in time can be too strong for any classical explanation -- and, remarkably, this can happen for a single quantum system measured twice, with no second particle involved. We show that when one qudit is sent through a noisy channel, the strength of this "nonlocality in time" -- the temporal nonlocality robustness $\mathrm{TNR}$ -- is carried entirely by the starting state: it vanishes precisely when the input is maximally mixed (completely random), $\mathrm{TNR}(ρ_A,\mathcal{E})=0\Leftrightarrowρ_A=\mathbb{1}/d$, for the standard noise families. The resource is not any coherence in the channel but the back-action of the input's mixedness, and it survives even complete decoherence. This is at once a power and a trap. As a power, $\mathrm{TNR}$ device-independently lower-bounds the fidelity of temporal teleportation -- sending an unknown state forward in time -- reaching $7/9$ at $d=3$, without trusting the measuring devices. As a trap, because the certified quantity is decoupled from the channel's actual coherence transmission, it can certify more than the channel delivers: an injective (reversible) unitary attains the maximal temporal-Bell signal yet teleports below the classical baseline. We resolve this over-certification completely -- a universal cap $\mathrm{TNR}\le(d-1)/d$ with an exact channel-resolved value, honest certification for the depolarizing channel and for any sufficiently mixed probe, and a proof that no choice of probes makes it channel-universal. Underpinning the results is a unified semidefinite-programming hierarchy of the temporal entanglement, steering and nonlocality robustnesses ($\mathrm{TER}$, $\mathrm{TSR}$, $\mathrm{TNR}$), with a strict lower hierarchy and an upper one conditional on no-signaling in time ($\mathrm{NSIT}$). All structure is verified numerically for $d=2$ through $5$.

</details>


### [151] [Kardar-Parisi-Zhang dynamics in an open integrable system: beyond the spontaneous-symmetry-breaking ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02341)
*Guo-Qiang Wang,Chang-Ling Zou,Guang-Can Guo,and Xu-Bo Zou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现开放可积B3模型中的电荷输运遵循Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ)动力学，而非基于自发对称性破缺(SSB)的唯象假设所预期的扩散行为，揭示了SSB假设的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于自发对称性破缺(SSB)的唯象假设被提出用于统一描述开放量子系统中的电荷输运，但该假设在何种条件下会失效、失效时是否会出现非平凡动力学尚不清楚。

Method: 通过将B3模型等价为两个相互作用的非对称XXZ自旋链，分析了SSB假设仅能捕捉链间相互作用的影响，而链内非对称XXZ结构在适当初始态下主导动力学。

Result: B3模型呈现KPZ标度行为而非SSB预期的扩散行为，且即使在跃迁率为负的情况下KPZ行为仍然存在。

Conclusion: 开放系统中的电荷输运理论需要超越基于SSB的唯象假设，以描述更丰富的动力学行为。

Abstract: The universality of dynamical scaling laws constitutes a cornerstone in the theoretical understanding of quantum many-body systems, particularly in non-equilibrium settings. Recent advancements have proposed a phenomenological ansatz based on spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) to unify the description of charge transport in open quantum systems. However, it remains unclear under which conditions it fails to capture the emergent hydrodynamics and if it does break down, whether nontrivial dynamics emerge. In this work we show that Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) dynamics in an open integrable model (the B3 model), rather than diffusion from SSB, emerges. We find that the B3 model is equivalent to two interacting asymmetric XXZ spin chains and the ansatz can only capture the influence of the inter-chain interactions. When the initial state is appropriate, the asymmetric XXZ structure dominates the dynamics, which gives KPZ scaling behavior even when the hopping rate becomes negative. Our work motivates theory of charge transport in open systems beyond the ansatz based on SSB.

</details>


### [152] [Recovery Algorithm for Correlated Errors in Permutation-Invariant Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02346)
*Omprakash Chandra,Yingkai Ouyang,Gopikrishnan Muraleedharan,Gavin Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子错误恢复(QER)方案，利用错误信道知识找到最优恢复映射，并结合置换不变(PI)码实现高性能量子纠错。针对对称振幅阻尼噪声，提出了新的CAD码系列，其中CAD9码比现有码性能提升一个数量级以上，CAD4码可完美纠正一个全局对称AD错误，恢复电路仅需10个门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子纠错码(如稳定子码)在非Pauli噪声(如振幅阻尼噪声)下需要大量额外开销，且恢复映射并非最优。作者希望利用错误信道的先验知识设计最优恢复映射，同时降低电路复杂度和寻址需求。

Method: 使用基于量子电路的通用相干QER映射，结合置换不变(PI)码实现。PI码具有可调参数以适应噪声模型，且恢复电路简单、寻址需求低。针对集体和局域对称关联振幅阻尼(AD)噪声建模，并提出新的CAD码系列。

Result: CAD9码(9量子比特)比现有码性能提升一个数量级以上；CAD4码(4量子比特)可完美纠正1个全局对称AD错误，恢复编译电路仅含10个系统及系统-辅助比特门，可通过线性几何相位门实现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条从优化恢复映射到实验可实现的低开销协议的直接路径，展示了QER在实际量子系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum Error Recovery (QER) uses knowledge of the error channel acting on a quantum system to find optimal recovery maps. The scheme restores the uncorrupted state with a fidelity exceeding that achieved by noise parameter independent quantum error correction. We use a generic coherent QER map implemented with a quantum circuit acting on the system together with ancillary qubits to recover quantum information stored in permutation invariant (PI) codes. PI codes admit tunable parameters to suit the noise model and benefit from simple recovery operation circuits with reduced addressability requirements, unlike stabilizer codes. We showcase the method by modeling QER in PI codes after collective and local symmetric correlated amplitude-damping (AD) noise, a non-Pauli noise process for which stabilizer codes often require additional overhead. We also propose a new PI code family called CAD codes with explicit examples on 4 and 9 qubits for global symmetric AD errors. We show that CAD9 (supported on 9 qubits) code beats many existing codes by more than one order of magnitude.
