For the CAD4 code, which perfectly corrects 1 global symmetric AD error, the compiled recovery circuit consists of 10 system and system-ancilla gates which can be realized from linear geometric phase gates. Our work provides a direct path from optimized recovery maps to experimentally implementable, low-overhead protocols.

</details>


### [153] [Stable Self-Modulating Quantum Fast-Weight Programmers with Bounded Memory Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02363)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chun-Hua Lin,Yifeng Peng,Junghoon Justin Park,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Hsin-Yi Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Chen-Yu Liu,Shinjae Yoo,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有界旧状态调制规则，用于量子快速权重编程器（QFWP）中的自调制变体，通过对循环记忆分支应用保号tanh门控来解决长序列中的发散问题，并在量子动力学预测和Milan SMS任务上验证了旧状态调制是核心机制，有界门控是有效的稳定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自调制QFWP中无界旧状态乘法器在长序列场景下会发散，需要一种稳定的有界调制方案来消除发散问题，同时保留自调制机制带来的性能提升。

Method: 提出有界旧状态调制规则：仅在循环记忆分支上应用保号tanh门控，保持加性更新和新更新调制不变。实验对比了标准QFWP、完整自调制QFWP、仅新调制和仅旧调制四种变体。

Result: 在量子动力学任务上，旧状态调制是相对标准QFWP最一致的改进来源，有界门控消除了长序列发散并提升了鲁棒性。在Milan SMS任务上，无源自调制QFWP在测试网格上收敛，在长输入窗口上增益最明显，行为接近仅旧调制消融。

Conclusion: 自调制QFWP的关键机制是累积记忆调制，有界旧状态门控是一种针对性的稳定化策略。

Abstract: Quantum Fast-Weight Programmers (QFWPs) store temporal information in dynamically programmed variational-circuit parameters rather than in nonlinear recurrent hidden states, offering a practical route to quantum sequence modeling. Self-Modulating QFWP improves this framework by using input-dependent gates for both new fast-weight updates and the accumulated fast-weight state, but its unbounded old-state multiplier can diverge in long-sequence regimes. We propose a bounded old-state modulation rule that applies a sign-preserving tanh gate only to the recurrent memory branch while leaving the additive update and new-update modulation unchanged. We evaluate standard QFWP, full Self-Modulating QFWP, Only-New, and Only-Old variants on two CUDA-Q quantum-dynamics forecasting tasks and on Milan SMS telecommunication activity prediction. The quantum-dynamics results show that old-state modulation is the most consistent source of improvement over Standard QFWP, and that bounding the old-state gate removes long-sequence divergence while improving aggregate robustness. On Milan SMS forecasting, the original unbounded Self-Modulating QFWP converges across the tested grid and shows its clearest gains at longer input windows, with behavior close to the Only-Old ablation. These findings identify accumulated-memory modulation as the key mechanism of Self-Modulating QFWP and bounded old-state gating as a targeted stabilization strategy.

</details>


### [154] [Copying Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02408)
*Hans Maassen,Burkhard Kümmerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子概率论框架（有限维C*-代数与单位完全正映射范畴）下讨论并证明了量子信息中的不可广播定理及其密切相关的不可克隆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信息理论中，不可广播定理和不可克隆定理是基本限制条件，但传统的证明多基于希尔伯特空间形式。本文希望在更一般的量子概率论框架（C*-代数范畴）下重新审视和证明这些定理，以揭示其更深层的数学结构。

Method: 采用范畴论方法，将量子系统建模为有限维C*-代数，将量子操作建模为单位完全正（UCP）映射，在这一框架下形式化并证明不可广播定理和不可克隆定理。

Result: 在C*-代数与UCP映射的范畴中，成功证明了不可广播定理：一组态允许共同广播操作当且仅当它们的密度矩阵属于交换族；同时也证明了不可克隆定理作为其推论。

Conclusion: 量子概率论框架为不可广播和不可克隆定理提供了更为统一和本质的数学理解，突显了这些量子信息限制与代数结构之间的内在联系。

Abstract: The no-broadcasting theorem in quantum information says that a set of states on a quantum system admits a common broadcasting (copying) operation if and only if their density matrices belong to a commuting family. We discuss and prove this theorem, as well as the closely related no-cloning theorem in the context of quantum probability theory, i.e. in the category of (finite dimensional) C-star-algebras with unital completely positive maps.

</details>


### [155] [Optimal stellar rank approximation of squeezed cat states with photon catalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02427)
*Julian K. Nauth,Nathan Walk,Ananga M. Datta,Kurt Busch,Jens Eisert,Oliver Benson,Roger A. Kögler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了光子催化（photon catalysis）技术在低阶Fock态与压缩态之间产生压缩相干态叠加（SCS）的性能，利用恒星秩（stellar rank）形式化方法量化非高斯资源复杂度，系统比较了催化输出态与目标态的保真度，并评估了实验损耗对态质量的影响，为近期的光子学实现提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯量子态和操作对于在玻色子平台上实现量子计算优势和量子纠错至关重要，然而在光学环境中生成非高斯态仍是一个挑战性的实验任务，目前多依赖于概率性的报知协议。本文旨在深入探究光子催化协议在生成压缩相干态叠加方面的适用性与最优性。

Method: 采用恒星秩（stellar rank）形式化方法表征输入资源（包括态和测量）及生成态的非高斯复杂度；系统比较催化输出态与目标态之间的保真度，并与相同非高斯输入资源下任何协议能达到的最大保真度进行对比；对光子催化与高斯玻色采样启发式协议在成功概率和态质量方面进行基准测试；通过Fock基希尔伯特空间截断方法模拟损耗，分析生成态在实验不完美条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 识别了光子催化协议可证明最优的实例；找到了以最小资源实现高保真近似目标态的参数区间；展示了确定性Fock态源在成功概率和态质量方面的优势；量化了非高斯资源复杂度、可实现保真度与损耗之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 光子催化是一种高效生成非高斯量子态（如压缩相干态叠加和压缩Fock态）的实用方案，研究结果为近期光子学实验实现提供了优化资源分配和参数选择的实用指南。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian quantum states and operations constitute essential resources for achieving quantum computational advantage and enabling quantum error correction in bosonic platforms. However, their generation in optical settings remains a challenging experimental task, often relying on probabilistic heralded protocols. Here, we present an in-depth analysis of the suitability of photon catalysis between low number Fock states and squeezed states for the generation of squeezed coherent state superpositions. We employ the stellar rank formalism to characterize the non-Gaussian complexity of input resources (including both states and measurements) and the generated states. This enables a systematic comparison of the fidelity between the catalyzed output and the target states to the maximum fidelity achievable by any protocol with the same non-Gaussian input resources. In this sense, we identify instances where the catalysis protocols considered here are provably optimal. We identify parameter regimes in which high-fidelity approximations of the target states can be achieved with minimal resources. Furthermore, we benchmark the performance of photon catalysis against Gaussian boson sampling-inspired protocols in terms of success probability and state quality, highlighting the advantages of deterministic Fock state sources. We also investigate the generation of related non-Gaussian resources including squeezed Fock states relevant for quantum error correction. To account for experimental imperfections, we model losses across all optical modes using a Hilbert space truncation approach in the Fock basis and analyze the robustness of the generated states under realistic conditions. Our results quantify the trade-offs between non-Gaussian resource complexity, achievable fidelity, and losses in photon catalysis protocols, providing practical guidelines for near-term photonic implementations.

</details>


### [156] [Optimal Stabilizer Testing and Learning with Limited Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02444)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Louis Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限相干量子记忆下的稳定子态测试与学习问题。结果表明，在内存受限时（即使保留0.99n qubits），稳定子态测试与学习之间原有的样本复杂度分离（测试需O(1)样本vs学习需Θ(n)样本）消失。测试复杂度为Θ(n-k)，非自适应学习复杂度为Θ(n²/k)，并给出纯度测试的指数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相干量子记忆资源在稳定子态测试与学习之间样本复杂度分离中的核心作用，明确记忆约束如何影响量子态测试与学习的根本性差异。

Method: 上界通过建立与hidden shift问题的联系实现；下界利用随机正交群组合学对似然比进行平均情况边界分析；纯度测试下界通过指数级论证完成。

Result: (1) 测试样本复杂度为Θ(n-k)；(2) 非自适应学习样本复杂度为Θ(n²/k)；(3) 纯度测试指数下界。关键发现：即使k=0.99n，稳定子态测试不再需要常数样本；当k=cn (0<c<1)时，测试与学习同样困难，均需Θ(n)样本。

Conclusion: 相干量子记忆是导致稳定子态测试与学习之间原有分离的关键资源。一旦记忆受限，这种分离消失，测试与学习的样本复杂度均随系统规模线性增长。

Abstract: We study stabilizer state testing and learning with limited coherent quantum memory. Here an algorithm sequentially receives copies of an unknown $n$-qubit state, but may keep only $k$ qubits of coherent quantum memory between measurements. With unrestricted memory, seminal work of Gross, Nezami and Walter showed how to test $n$-qubit stabilizer states using $6$ copies, which is dimension independent, unlike the learning complexity of $Θ(n)$. We show that this testing-vs-learning separation is lost under memory constraints. More concretely we show that
  (1) The sample complexity of testing stabilizer states in the $k$-qubit memory framework is $Θ(n-k)$. Our upper bound goes via a novel connection to the hidden shift problem and the lower bound is proven using a novel approach to average case bounds on likelihood ratios via combinatorics of the stochastic orthogonal group.
  (2) The sample complexity of learning stabilizer states with $k$ qubits of memory, in the non-adaptive framework, is $Θ(n^2/k)$.
  As a further application of our techniques, we prove an exponential lower bound for purity testing even when the memory may be left coherent throughout the protocol. Our main results identify coherent quantum memory as the resource enabling the usual separation between stabilizer testing and learning. In particular, even with $k=0.99n$ qubits of memory, there is no constant-copy stabilizer tester; furthermore for $k=cn$ qubits of memory (for $0< c < 1$), stabilizer testing is as hard as learning, with both requiring $Θ(n)$ copies.

</details>


### [157] [Symmetries of Pauli Noise from Lindbladian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02481)
*Moein Malekakhlagh,Edward H. Chen,Luke C. G. Govia,Alireza Seif*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子噪声的物理结构对Pa利保真度施加了近似对称性约束，利用这些对称性可以在不依赖误差幅度的条件下固定规范，从而区分SPAM误差，并通过Lindbladian微扰理论推导了不同误差类型的对称性破缺选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路噪声表征受到规范自由度的根本限制——SPAM误差的个体贡献在门集实验中原则上不可区分。作者希望通过利用实际噪声的物理结构来打破这一限制。

Method: 利用Lindbladian微扰理论分析Clifford门族（ZZ_{π/2}, CZ, CNOT, iSWAP, SWAP），推导Pauli保利真度在门共轭变换下的对称性；通过数值和IBM Kingston实验验证。

Result: 相干误差不产生一阶不对称性；仅特定非对角耗散误差能在首阶破缺对称性；常见单量子比特噪声（T1弛豫、T2φ纯退相干）仅能引起二阶不对称性。

Conclusion: 利用噪声物理对称性固定规范可系统识别SPAM误差，简化误差表征与缓解流程。

Abstract: Characterizing noise in quantum circuits is fundamentally limited by gauge degrees of freedom; certain parameters, such as the individual contributions of state preparation and measurement (SPAM) errors, are in principle unlearnable from any experiment within the gate set. Here, we show that the physical structure of realistic noise processes imposes approximate symmetry constraints on the Pauli fidelities of gate noise channels. These symmetries relate the fidelity of a Pauli $P$ and its gate-conjugate $U_g P U_g ^{\dagger}$, and can be used to fix the gauge using only knowledge of the error type and not its magnitude. Using Lindbladian perturbation theory, we analyze a broad class of Clifford gates, including $ZZ_{π/2}$, CZ, CNOT, iSWAP, and SWAP, and demonstrate that coherent errors do not induce first-order asymmetry, while only a restricted set of predominantly off-diagonal dissipative errors can break the symmetry at first order, for which we derive simple selection rules. Notably, common single-qubit noise sources such as $T_1$-relaxation and $T_{2φ}$-pure-dephasing can only cause asymmetry at second order. Leveraging these symmetries to fix the gauge enables systematic identification of SPAM errors, simplifying error characterization and mitigation. We validate our results numerically and experimentally on IBM Kingston.

</details>


### [158] [Automated logical Clifford gadgets for heterogeneous architectures via chain maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02482)
*Asmae Benhemou,Noah Berthusen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化框架，利用链映射在任意CSS码对之间合成逻辑CNOT电路，能够搜索浅层稀疏的物理电路实现，并扩展到CZ门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同CSS码之间的逻辑CNOT操作选项非常有限，通常仅适用于结构相关的码族，缺乏通用的自动化构造方法。

Method: 基于链映射（chain maps）的自动化框架：给定二分逻辑CNOT网络，构造实现所需逻辑作用的链映射的仿射空间，并搜索浅层稀疏的物理电路候选。

Result: 在多种异构CSS码对上基准测试，恢复了已知的横向构造，发现了新的低深度解（包括距离保持和部分距离保持的例子），可通过额外flag测量提升至完整码距离。

Conclusion: 该框架可应用于码切换、魔法态注入、Pauli乘积测量及级联码操作，为异构架构的逻辑接口提供有利的时空权衡，并可直接扩展至逻辑CZ门。

Abstract: Transversal CNOTs are ubiquitous for entangling logical qubits of identical CSS codes pairwise. For distinct codes, the options are much more limited, and are typically known only for structurally related code families. We introduce an automated framework for synthesising inter-code logical CNOT circuits between arbitrary CSS codes using chain maps. Given a prescribed bipartite logical CNOT network between these codes, our method constructs the affine space of chain maps realising the desired logical action, and then searches this space for shallow and sparse physical circuit candidates. We benchmark this method on a range of heterogeneous CSS code pairs, recovering known transversal constructions, and finding new low-depth solutions, including distance-preserving and partially distance-preserving examples, which we demonstrate can be promoted to the full code distance using additional flag measurements. We discuss applications to code switching, magic-state injection, Pauli product measurements, and operations on concatenated codes, where bespoke chain maps offer favourable spacetime tradeoffs for logical interfaces tailored to heterogeneous architectures. Finally, we show how our framework straightforwardly extends to targeted logical CZ gates.

</details>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Dirac oscillator in a helically twisted spacetime with axial torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01301)
*Matheus D. Moro,Fabiano M. Andrade,Faizuddin Ahmed,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究扭折时空中的狄拉克谐振子，推导了包含轴向扭转和螺旋扭曲的径向动力学方程，发现螺旋扭曲会二次方地消除超对称零模，并给出了能谱和热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在具有均匀轴向扭转的螺旋扭曲时空中，狄拉克谐振子的量子动力学行为，特别是几何效应（自旋连接、度规非对角项）如何影响能谱对称性和零模结构。

Method: 从正交余标架出发，显式计算Levi-Civita自旋连接，分离轴向contortion的几何贡献；保留径向Moshinsky耦合中的β矩阵，将二阶问题表述为有序乘积算子；使用有限元方法数值求解径向耦合二分量系统。

Result: 在平坦极限下复现了平面狄拉克谐振子能谱；观察到对纵向动量的不对称依赖、能级回避交叉以及E=Mc²处的超对称零模；轴向扭转和纵向动量保持零模，而螺旋扭曲二次方地提升零模；给出了分扇区的热力学函数、纵向矢量流和轴矢量流，以及Witten指标分析。

Conclusion: 螺旋扭曲是解除超对称零模的关键形变参数，该框架为弯曲时空中的相对论量子束缚态和拓扑量子计算提供了可解析处理的理论平台。

Abstract: We investigate the Dirac oscillator in a helically twisted spacetime endowed with a uniform axial torsion. Starting from an orthonormal coframe, we compute the Levi--Civita spin connection explicitly and separate the geometric contribution from the axial contortion. Retaining the matrix $β$ in the radial Moshinsky coupling, we show that the second-order problem is the ordered product $\hatΠ_+\hatΠ_-$ rather than the square of a single operator. The resulting radial dynamics is a coupled, self-adjoint two-component system in which the spin connection supplies the correct cylindrical radial operator, while the off-diagonal metric generates the helical combination $m/r-ωk$ and a Coulomb-like geometric term. A finite-element solution reproduces the planar Dirac-oscillator spectrum in the flat limit and reveals asymmetric dependence on the longitudinal momentum, avoided level crossings, and a supersymmetric zero mode at $E=Mc^2$. The axial torsion and longitudinal momentum preserve this zero mode, whereas the helical twist lifts it quadratically. Sector-resolved thermodynamic functions are obtained from the relativistic bound-state spectrum. The explicit spinors further determine longitudinal vector and axial currents, and a Witten-index analysis identifies the helical twist as the deformation that removes the protected zero mode.

</details>


### [160] [What's the Matter with 3D Gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01319)

Method: 采用世界线形式构建经典相空间，利用几何量子化进行量子化；通过等变局域化方法计算热AdS₃上的配分函数。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of minimally coupling matter to Einstein gravity in three dimensions with negative cosmological constant. By working in the worldline formalism, we construct a classical phase space on an initial time surface $Σ$, which we quantize using geometric quantization. States in the Hilbert space correspond to Virasoro conformal blocks with operators of conformal weight $h<c/24$. As an application of our formalism, we compute the partition function on thermal $\text{AdS}_3$ through equivariant localization. Our answer reproduces the AdS$_3$ Wilson spool and agrees with the known one-loop result. It further serves as a conjecture for the value of the path integral of gravity minimally coupled to a massive scalar field in thermal $\text{AdS}_3$ to all orders in $G_N$.

</details>


### [161] [The Art of Networking: Networks of Trivalent 10d Heterotic Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01321)

Method: 从三种10维非快子异质弦理论的交界点出发，将其(0,1)异质弦世界片描述推广到任意网络；利用图论对一维网络进行拓扑形式化，并提供世界片实现；进而扩展到高维网络（如气泡成核），并讨论紧致构型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了弦理论网络的一般框架，揭示了共形猜测下理论间交界点的丰富结构，并为在量子几何（如S^1 ∨ S^1）上紧致化提供了新见解。

Abstract: We initiate the study of networks of 10d string theories connected by junctions implied by the cobordism conjecture. Focusing on the recently constructed junction of the three 10d non-tachyonic heterotic theories, we generalize its $(0, 1)$ heterotic worldsheet description to construct arbitrary networks. For one-dimensional networks, we formulate their topology in terms of graph theory and provide a simple worldsheet realization for general graphs. We then extend our analysis to higher-dimensional networks, describing e.g. nucleation in a theory of bubbles of pairs of other theories. We also discuss compact configurations, which define a novel class of compactifications in which different sectors propagate on different compact spaces, in a way reminiscent of compactifications on quantum geometries like $S^1 \vee S^1$.

</details>


### [162] [Wormholes as red herrings: reflection positivity and the reconstruction of unitary quantum field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01322)
*Jacob McNamara,Zhencheng Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，量子引力中因空间虫洞（爱因斯坦-罗森桥）导致的希尔伯特空间分解表观失效，实为红鲱鱼现象，源于对荷空间的不完全采样；通过建立QFT重构定理，证明幺正QFT由闭流形配分函数唯一确定，且反射正配分函数必对应幺正QFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子引力中空间虫洞（爱因斯坦-罗森桥）导致希尔伯特空间分解表观失效的谜题，类比Coleman对欧几里得虫洞导致配分函数因子化失效的解释。

Method: 建立量子场论重构定理：(1) 幺正QFT由闭流形配分函数唯一确定（至多幺正同构）；(2) 每个反射正配分函数对应一个幺正QFT；(3) 流形制备的态张成重构理论对称群下的不变态空间。

Result: 空间虫洞导致的希尔伯特空间分解表观失效是红鲱鱼，根源在于限制在不完全的荷谱上，而非量子引力本质非局域性。

Conclusion: 希尔伯特空间分解的表观失效与Coleman的论证类似，并非量子引力的根本问题，而是因为忽略了荷空间的完整结构。

Abstract: As Coleman famously argued, the apparent breakdown of partition-function factorization in quantum gravity associated with Euclidean wormholes is a red herring, arising from a hidden average over an ensemble of theories. We present a direct analog of Coleman's argument for the apparent breakdown of Hilbert-space factorization associated with spatial wormholes, i.e., Einstein--Rosen bridges. Our main result is the following reconstruction theorem for quantum field theories: unitary QFTs are determined, up to unitary isomorphism, by their closed-manifold partition functions; every reflection-positive partition function arises from a unitary quantum field theory; and the states prepared by manifolds span the space of invariant states under the reconstructed theory's symmetry group. Interpreting the result gravitationally, we conclude that any apparent breakdown of Hilbert-space factorization is a red herring, arising from restricting to an incomplete spectrum of charged states.

</details>


### [163] [Algorithmic Dualization of Unitary Circular Quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01327)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性箭图的SL(2,ℤ)对偶网络算法推广到圆形箭图理论，处理圆形拓扑带来的额外复杂性，并研究“坏”圆形箭图的对偶性质。

Method: 引入拓扑与重子数QFT模块以及新的SL(2,ℤ)对偶操作，构建圆形箭图的场论对偶算法；区分局部坏性（规范节点欠平衡）和全局坏性（源自圆形拓扑本身），通过磁电对偶框架和指标计算验证对偶性。

Result: 成功将线性箭图的对偶算法扩展到圆形箭图，给出了好圆形箭图镜像对称性的场论推导；对坏圆形箭图，识别了局部与全局坏性，并通过Higgs支与Coulomb支指标的匹配为全局坏圆形箭图的对偶提供了额外证据。

Conclusion: 圆形箭图理论具有丰富的新对偶结构，全局坏性会导致类似置换群规范的结构，并揭示了与ADHM箭图和𝒩=8红外不动点的精细关系。

Abstract: We introduce a field-theoretic algorithm to find the $SL(2,\mathbb{Z})$ duality web of 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ circular quiver theories with unitary gauge groups, extending the algorithm for linear quivers. Although circular and linear quivers share the same local structure, the circular topology requires additional ingredients, which we formulate in terms of topological and baryonic QFT blocks, together with new $SL(2,\mathbb{Z})$ duality moves acting on them. For good circular quivers, this provides a field-theoretic derivation of mirror symmetry and extends it to the full $SL(2,\mathbb{Z})$ duality web. We then study bad circular quivers, distinguishing between local badness, associated with under-balanced gauge nodes, and global badness, arising from the circular topology itself. In particular, we analyze the magnetic and electric dual frames of globally bad circular quivers and provide additional evidence for the proposed duality by matching the Higgs branch index with the dual Coulomb branch index. The latter exhibits a structure reminiscent of permutation-group gauging and reveals a refined relation to the ADHM quiver, flowing to the $\mathcal{N}=8$ infrared fixed point.

</details>


### [164] [Quark Anti-Quark Fusion and Walking RG Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01328)
*Filipp Chernikov,Nikolay Gromov,Amit Sever*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了球面上两个共轭共形线缺陷的融合现象。在小间距下，其谱由普适的融合主方程控制。低于临界耦合时，融合缺陷有两个共形固定点；在临界点处它们碰撞并进入复平面，产生行走RG行为。尽管能级随UV尺度漂移且依赖方案，但SL(2,R) Casimir算符在该尺度以下仍与哈密顿量对易，从而将谱组织成共形族并确定普适的态密度。作者在平面梯子模型中推导了这一结构，并利用量子谱曲线获得了平面N=4 SYM中共轭1/2-BPS Wilson线融合的精确有限耦合描述，最后通过微扰论和半经典弦理论进行了检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形场论中线缺陷融合的普适行为，特别是融合过程中谱结构和重整化群流动的性质。

Method: 采用融合主方程分析方法，结合平面梯子模型和N=4 SYM中的量子谱曲线（QSC）方法，辅以微扰论和半经典弦理论验证。

Result: 发现了低于临界耦合时的两个共形固定点、临界点的行走RG行为，以及SL(2,R) Casimir算符在缺陷融合后对谱结构的组织作用，并获得了普适的态密度。

Conclusion: 共轭共形线缺陷融合展现出丰富的RG结构，且存在一个普适的、与方案无关的态密度，该结构在梯子模型和N=4 SYM中得到了精确描述和验证。

Abstract: We study the fusion of two conjugate conformal line defects on the sphere. At small separation, their spectrum is governed by a universal Fusion Master Equation. Below a critical coupling, the fused defect has two conformal fixed points; at criticality, they collide and move into the complex plane, producing walking RG behaviour. Although individual energy levels then drift with the UV scale and are scheme dependent, the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ Casimir continues to commute with the Hamiltonian below that scale. This organises the spectrum into conformal families and fixes a universal, scheme-independent density of states. We derive this structure in the planar ladder model and obtain an exact finite-coupling description of conjugate $1/2$-BPS Wilson-line fusion in planar ${\cal N}=4$ SYM using the Quantum Spectral Curve. We test our results against perturbation theory and semiclassical string theory.

</details>


### [165] [Wigner negativity in Krylov space and emergent semiclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01351)
*Vijay Balasubramanian,Pawel Caputa,Onkar Parrikar,Vivek Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出Krylov基为大N复杂多体系统提供了半经典表示。通过研究Wigner负性（经典模拟复杂性的度量）在Krylov空间中的增长，发现在2d CFT中Wigner负性保持O(1)常数；随机矩阵理论中呈t^{1/2}增长但不随Hilbert空间维度缩放；双标度SYK模型中早期为常数相，后期为t^{1/2}增长相。这些结果表明Krylov空间中的动力学具有涌现的半经典性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Krylov基能否为大N复杂多体系统提供半经典动力学表示，并通过Wigner负性增长来量化这种半经典性的涌现。

Method: 在Krylov空间中研究Wigner负性随时间演化的行为，分别在2d CFT（真空态和热场双态）、随机矩阵理论（最大纠缠初态）和双标度SYK模型中进行解析计算和分析。

Result: 2d CFT中Wigner负性为O(1)常数不增长；随机矩阵理论中Wigner负性以t^{1/2}增长但不随Hilbert空间维度缩放；双标度SYK模型中早期为常数相，后期进入t^{1/2}增长相。

Conclusion: Krylov空间中的动力学在所有研究模型中均呈现涌现的半经典性，表现为Wigner负性要么保持常数要么缓慢增长，说明Krylov基为大N复杂系统提供了有效的半经典描述。

Abstract: We propose that the Krylov basis gives a semiclassical representation of dynamics in general large-$N$, complex, many-body systems. As a probe of this semiclassicality, we study the growth of Wigner negativity -- a measure of the complexity of classical simulation -- under time evolution in Krylov space in several solvable models. We begin with 2d CFTs, initially in either the vacuum or the thermofield double state on a line excited by a primary operator. In both cases, Wigner negativity remains an $O(1)$ constant and does not grow at late times, indicating approximately classical dynamics in the Krylov basis. We then study random matrix theory with the maximally entangled state between two copies as the initial state. For general one-cut matrix models, we argue that Wigner negativity in the Krylov basis grows as $t^{1/2}$ at large $O(1)$ times but does not scale with the Hilbert space dimension, thus indicating semiclassical dynamics in Krylov space. Finally, in the double-scaled SYK model, we find an approximately classical phase (constant negativity) at early times and a semiclassical phase ($t^{1/2}$ growth) at late times. In all these examples, Wigner negativity either remains constant or grows slowly, demonstrating emergent semiclassicality of dynamics in Krylov space.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum JT Gravity in a box as a Pöschl-Teller Scattering Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01385)
*Luca Griguolo,Jacopo Papalini,Lorenzo Russo,Domenico Seminara,Alex Tarana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对带有限狄利克雷边界条件的Jackiw-Teitelboim引力进行了正则量子化，利用两个边界之间的测地线长度及其共轭动量作为约化相空间变量，将动力学重新表述为排斥Pöschl-Teller势中的非相对论粒子散射问题，获得了精确的宇宙波函数和盘配分函数，并发现有限截止下的谱测度存在现有T\bar{T}方法中缺失的非微扰修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限截止下对JT引力进行精确量子化，特别是超越现有T\bar{T}变形方法，捕捉非微扰修正，并解决准局部能的分支割奇异性以及黑洞内部结构的量子描述问题。

Method: 采用测地线长度及其共轭动量作为约化相空间变量进行正则量子化；利用双曲约化将动力学嵌入sl(2,R)卡西米尔不变量；将问题映射为Pöschl-Teller势散射问题；对布朗-约克荷进行解析延拓以穿越黑洞视界。

Result: 获得了精确的宇宙波函数和盘配分函数；在渐近极限下重现标准Schwarzian谱密度；在有限截止下得到了现有T\bar{T}方法中不存在的非微扰修正；获得了热两点函数的闭式表达式（Wilson函数）以及四点函数的费曼规则；提出了通过解析延拓布朗-约克荷实现模型UV完备化的方案。

Conclusion: 该正则量子化方案为有限截止JT引力提供了超越微扰论的非微扰描述，并自然地将散射问题扩展到黑洞内部区域，为理解黑洞信息问题提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We present a canonical quantization of Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity with finite Dirichlet boundary conditions, using the geodesic length between the two boundaries and its conjugate momentum as reduced phase space variables. The dynamics is recast as the scattering problem of a nonrelativistic particle in a repulsive Pöschl-Teller potential, naturally embedded within a hyperbolic reduction of the $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$ Casimir. We obtain exact wavefunctions of the universe and the disk partition function, interpreted as a transition matrix element between states of vanishing bare length. In the asymptotic limit, the theory reduces to Liouville quantum mechanics and reproduces the standard Schwarzian spectral density. At finite cutoff, however, the spectral measure exhibits genuinely nonperturbative corrections, absent in existing $T\bar T$ treatments. We also obtain closed form expressions for thermal two-point functions in terms of Wilson functions and propose diagrammatic rules for time- and out-of-time-ordered four-point functions. We further address the issue of the branch cut singularity of the quasi-local energy and propose a UV completion of the model in which the Brown-York charge is analytically continued beyond the black hole horizon. This continuation naturally extends the scattering problem to configurations that foliate the black hole interior.

</details>


### [167] [Preparing a Thermofield Double State with Feedback Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01653)
*Guilherme E. L. Pexe,Lucas A. M. Rattighieri,Felipe F. Fanchini,Dario Rosa,Amilson R. Fritsch*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究利用反馈式量子算法制备Maldacena-Qi模型（双耦合SYK模型）的热场双态（TFD），并提出混合ITE-TR-FALQON协议，通过引入非酉动力学和时间重标定克服标准方法的动力学限制，成功高保真度制备了高度纠缠的基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联热态（如TFD态）的高效制备是量子处理器上模拟量子引力模型和多体热力学的基本前提，现有反馈式量子算法在Maldacena-Qi模型中面临严重的动力学限制，无法从平凡积态收敛到高度纠缠基态。

Method: 提出混合ITE-TR-FALQON协议，融合虚时演化增强的FALQON（ITE-FALQON）中的虚时演化机制与时间重标定机制（TR-FALQON），通过非酉动力学打破对称性陷阱并滤除激发态，同时利用时间重标定加速算法收敛。

Result: 标准FALQON和TR-FALQON无法收敛到高度纠缠基态；混合ITE-TR-FALQON实现了接近单位保真度，并高精度复现了精确TFD态的von Neumann熵和Rényi熵谱。

Conclusion: 非酉动力学的引入是打破对称性陷阱和滤除激发态的必要条件，而时间重标定可显著加速算法收敛，该混合协议为量子引力模型和多体热力学模拟提供了可行的量子计算方案。

Abstract: The efficient preparation of correlated thermal states, such as the Thermofield Double (TFD) state, is a fundamental prerequisite for simulating quantum gravity models and many-body thermodynamics on quantum processors. In this work, we investigate the ground state preparation of the Two Coupled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, known as the Maldacena-Qi model, which is dual to a traversable wormhole in $AdS_2$, utilizing feedback-based quantum algorithms. We demonstrate that the standard feedback-based quantum algorithm (FALQON) and its time-rescaled variant (TR-FALQON) face severe kinetic limitations in this system, failing to converge to the highly entangled ground state when initialized in trivial product states. To overcome these barriers, we propose the hybrid ITE-TR-FALQON protocol, which integrates the imaginary-time evolution present in imaginary-time-enhanced FALQON (ITE-FALQON) with the time-rescaling mechanism. Our numerical results indicate that the introduction of non-unitary dynamics is strictly necessary to break symmetry traps and filter out excited states, while time-rescaling drastically accelerates algorithm convergence. The proposed method achieves fidelities close to unity and reproduces the von Neumann and Rényi entropy spectra of the exact TFD state with high precision.

</details>


### [168] [Morse Bridge between Planar Kepler and Hyperbolic Landau Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01778)
*Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了平面Kepler-Coulomb问题与双曲平面$H^2$上的Landau问题通过一个共同的一维中介——Morse哈密顿量相互关联。通过Liouville变换和耦合常数 metamorphosis，Kepler径向动力学转化为Morse问题；而双曲磁场动力学的horocyclic约简则给出相同的Morse哈密顿量，并带有量子半密度修正。由此，Kepler径向问题与双曲Landau问题的固定horocyclic动量扇区映射到同一Morse谱问题，关联了它们的束缚谱、连续谱阈值、共振和散射数据。此外，Landau时间演化呈现Kepler圆锥曲线形式，并约化为Morse系统的束缚、阈值和散射轨迹。建立的词典将Kepler圆锥曲线与磁圆、horocycle和hypercycle联系起来，并将Landau问题的磁$SL(2,\mathbb R)$对称性转化为Morse系统的谱生成代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索看似独立的Kepler-Coulomb问题与双曲Landau问题之间的深层联系，揭示它们通过Morse哈密顿量这一共同一维中介统一起来的数学结构。

Method: 采用Liouville变换和耦合常数metamorphosis将Kepler径向动力学映射为Morse问题；利用horocyclic约简将双曲平面上的Landau问题化为同一Morse哈密顿量；通过比较两者谱问题建立对应词典。

Result: Kepler径向问题与双曲Landau问题的固定horocyclic动量扇区映射到同一Morse谱问题；Landau时间演化具有Kepler圆锥曲线形式；建立了Kepler圆锥曲线与磁圆、horocycle、hypercycle之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: Kepler-Coulomb问题与双曲Landau问题通过Morse哈密顿量统一，两系统的物理量（谱、轨迹、对称性）之间存在精确对应，揭示了不同物理系统间深刻的数学等价性。

Abstract: We show that two paradigmatic systems, the planar Kepler--Coulomb problem and the Landau problem on the hyperbolic plane $H^2$, are connected by a common one-dimensional mediator: the Morse Hamiltonian. On the Kepler side, a Liouville transformation and coupling-constant metamorphosis turn the radial dynamics into the Morse problem, with the Kepler polar angle becoming the Morse evolution parameter. On the Landau side, horocyclic reduction of the hyperbolic magnetic dynamics gives the same Morse Hamiltonian, with a quantum half-density correction. Consequently, the radial Kepler problem and the fixed-horocyclic-momentum sectors of the hyperbolic Landau problem are mapped to one Morse spectral problem, relating their bound spectra, continuum thresholds, resonances and scattering data. We further show that the Landau time evolution has a Kepler-conic form and reduces to the bound, threshold and scattering trajectories of the Morse system. The resulting dictionary connects Kepler conics with magnetic circles, horocycles and hypercycles, and turns the magnetic $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry of the Landau problem into the spectrum-generating algebraic structure of the Morse system.

</details>


### [169] [Uniqueness and Analytic Structures of Bosonic String Effective Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01790)
*Qu Cao,Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了玻色弦理论中自旋-1外部态的有效振幅的零超越性扇区，该扇区被推测对应于质量变形的$(DF)^2$理论（即$(DF)^2+YM$理论）。通过施加规范不变性、局域性和循环性等最小假设，唯一确定了维度提升算符集，并建立了从杨-米尔斯振幅出发的递归构造。在有限$α'$下，由规范不变且依赖于$α'$的因子修饰的导数算符（称为逆算符）重构了完整的玻色弦有效振幅，得到简洁表达式，这些表达式普遍分解为快子极点系数乘以杨-米尔斯-标量振幅。该结构适用于任意重数，并扩展到纯$(DF)^2$、$(DF)^2+φ^3$和$(DF)^2+YM+φ^3$理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究玻色弦理论中自旋-1外部态有效振幅的零超越性扇区，验证其与质量变形的$(DF)^2$理论的对应关系，并建立从杨-米尔斯振幅出发的系统性构造方法。

Method: 通过施加规范不变性、局域性和循环性等最小假设唯一确定维度提升算符集；引入由规范不变且依赖于$α'$的因子修饰的导数算符（逆算符）来重构振幅；利用快子极点系数与杨-米尔斯-标量振幅的分解结构。

Result: 成功建立了从杨-米尔斯振幅到$(DF)^2+YM$理论振幅的递归构造；得到了振幅的紧凑表达式，该表达式分解为快子极点系数乘以杨-米尔斯-标量振幅；该结构适用于任意重数并扩展到多种相关理论。

Conclusion: 零超越性扇区的玻色弦有效振幅与$(DF)^2+YM$理论在结构上一致，通过逆算符方法可系统性地构建振幅，且该框架具有普适性，可推广到多种相关理论。

Abstract: We revisit the zero-transcendentality sector of bosonic string effective amplitudes with spin-1 external states, conjectured to correspond to a mass-deformed $(DF)^2$ theory, known as the $(DF)^2{+}\text{YM}$ theory. Imposing gauge invariance, locality, and cyclicity under minimal assumptions uniquely fixes a set of dimension-raising operators and leads to a recursive construction of amplitudes from Yang-Mills amplitudes in the $α'{\to}0$ limit. At finite $α'$, certain derivative operators dressed with gauge invariant and $α'$-dependent factors, what we call $\textit{inverse operators}$, reconstruct the full bosonic string effective amplitudes, yielding compact expressions that universally factorize into tachyon-pole coefficients times Yang-Mills-Scalar amplitudes. This structure holds at arbitrary multiplicity and also extends to the amplitudes of the pure $(DF)^2$, $(DF)^2{+}φ^{3}$ and $(DF)^2{+}\text{YM}{+}φ^{3}$ theories.

</details>


### [170] [Lubkin-Page typicality bounds for Type~II von~Neumann factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01873)
*Zhi-Wei Wang,Samuel L. Braunstein*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了Lubkin-Page典型性论证在Type II von Neumann代数中成立，即子系统间互信息在小维度比下消失；对Type II$_\infty$因子（包括引力代数）有指数抑制，并讨论了向Type III推广的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论与量子引力中的可观测量子代数通常是Type II或Type III，而Lubkin-Page边界基于Type I张量积Hilbert空间，因此需要检验该典型性论证是否适用于更一般的代数结构。

Method: 对于超有限Type II$_1$因子，利用有限维近似证明互信息按$O((d_A d_B / d_E)^2)$消失；对于Type II$_\infty$因子（包括Witten等人通过交叉积构造的引力代数），证明存在额外的指数抑制项，与Bekenstein-Hawking熵相关。

Result: Type II von Neumann因子中Lubkin-Page边界仍然成立：Type II$_1$因子中互信息按维度比平方消失，Type II$_\infty$因子中还有与Bekenstein-Hawking熵相关的指数抑制。Type III因子的推广存在障碍。

Conclusion: 典型性论证可推广至Type II代数，支持涌现时空的典型性观点；Type III情形仍需进一步研究，其中可观测代数的交换子是否可作为自然热浴以加强边界是开放问题。

Abstract: Typicality arguments for emergent spacetime rely on the Lubkin-Page bounds, which show that generic quantum states have vanishing correlations between subsystems. These bounds assume a tensor-product Hilbert space (a Type~I von~Neumann algebra), but the observable algebras in quantum field theory and quantum gravity are generically Type~II or Type~III, raising the question of whether the bounds survive. We prove that they do for all Type~II von~Neumann factors. For the hyperfinite Type~II$_1$ factor with a tripartite decomposition $R \cong A \otimes B \otimes E$, the mutual information between subsystems $A$ and $B$ vanishes as $O((d_A d_B / d_E)^2)$ in finite-dimensional approximations, provided $d_A d_B \leq d_E$ (Theorem~1). For Type~II$_\infty$ factors, including the gravitational algebras constructed via the crossed-product method by Witten and by Chandrasekaran, Longo, Penington, and Witten, the bound acquires an additional exponential suppression controlled by the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy (Theorem~2). We identify the obstructions to extending the result to Type~III factors and discuss the open question of whether the commutant of the observable algebra can serve as a natural thermal bath that tightens the bound further.

</details>


### [171] [Tuning quantum magic of pure quantum chaotic states with a gravity dual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01930)
*Antonio M. García-García,Xianlong Liu,Jie-ping Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）哈密顿量构造的量子态的量子魔法（quantum magic）资源，证明了在大N极限下纯KM态的量子魔法与N呈线性关系，斜率可在0到1/2之间调节；高斯态经实时演化后其FAF以指数速率趋近N/2；SYK能量本征态的次领头修正随N指数衰减，但在稀疏耦合下呈幂律衰减。这些结果为量子信息、量子混沌与低维量子引力之间的关系提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子魔法是衡量量子态能否被经典计算机高效模拟的基本资源，研究其在SYK模型构造的态中的表现，有助于理解量子信息、量子混沌与低维量子引力之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用解析方法在大N极限下分析纯KM态的量子魔法；对于高斯态的实时演化，利用Ruelle-Pollicot共振描述其FAF的指数趋近行为；对于SYK能量本征态，结合Krylov子空间与GPU加速技术对N≤54的系统进行数值计算。

Result: 纯KM态的量子魔法与N呈线性关系，斜率取决于黑洞温度，可在0到1/2之间调节；高斯态实时演化后FAF以指数速率趋近N/2；SYK能量本征态的次领头修正随N指数衰减，稀疏耦合下呈幂律衰减，且靠近基态的态修正量级更大。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了SYK模型中量子魔法的标度行为与可调性，为量子信息、量子混沌与低维量子引力之间的关系提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: Quantum magic is a fundamental resource that quantifies to what extent quantum states can be efficiently simulated on a classical computer. We study it for states constructed from the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian with $N$ Majoranas by the fermionic anti-flatness (FAF). We show analytically that, in the large $N$ limit, the quantum magic of pure Kourkoulou-Maldacena (KM) states, dual to a quantum black hole with an end-of-world particle behind the horizon, is linear in $N$ with a slope, depending on the black hole temperature, that can be tuned between zero and $1/2$. By contrast, the FAF of Gaussian states evolved in real time with the SYK Hamitonian approaches $\approx N/2$ exponentially at a rate given by a multiple of the leading Ruelle-Pollicot resonance. Subleading corrections in $N$ for SYK energy eigenstates, computed numerically for $N \leq 54$ by combining Krylov subspace with GPU acceleration techniques, decay exponentially with $N$, but power-law if the SYK couplings are sparsified, and are order of magnitude larger for states close to the ground state, a region with an established gravity analogue. Our results offer new insights about the relation between quantum information, quantum chaos and low-dimension quantum gravity.

</details>


### [172] [Local symmetry and the dependence on extended spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01999)
*Keith Glennon,Peter West*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性化E理论中的局部对称性，发现其参数需满足微分条件以限制对扩展时空的依赖。在约化为Siegel理论（即双场理论）时，参数需满足不同于标准截面条件的约束，且Siegel理论的膨胀子方程在参数满足非线性约束时具有不变性。作者认为无需对场施加额外条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究E理论中局部对称性的存在条件及其参数约束，澄清与双场理论中标准截面条件的区别，论证无需对场施加额外限制。

Method: 通过线性化分析E理论，推导局部对称性参数需满足的微分条件；将E理论分解为Siegel理论（双场理论），对比参数约束与标准截面条件的差异；检验膨胀子方程在非线性参数约束下的不变性。

Result: E理论低阶局部对称性参数满足微分条件；在Siegel理论中参数约束不同于标准截面条件；膨胀子方程在非线性参数约束下保持不变。

Conclusion: E理论与Siegel理论的局部对称性由参数约束决定，无需对场施加截面条件之类的额外限制。

Abstract: We show that linearised E theory possesses a local symmetry at low levels provided the parameters of the local symmetry obey differential conditions that restrict their dependence on the extended spacetime. In the decomposition of E theory that leads to Siegel theory, also known as Double Field theory, we also find the analogous restrictions on the parameters. They are different to the section conditions which are universally used in this context. We also show that the dilaton equation of Siegel theory is invariant under the local symmetry if the parameters satisfy an analogous non-linear constraint on the parameters. We argue that there is no need to impose conditions on the fields of E theory or Siegel theory.

</details>


### [173] [Towards graviton lasing from squeezed ultra-cold systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02068)
*Soham Sen,Vlatko Vedral*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出利用带电谐振子阵列在动态电磁场中有效探测引力子，并基于超冷原子系统中的布居反转机制，提出了一种产生引力子激光的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力子的直接探测是量子引力领域的核心难题，现有方法灵敏度不足，亟需新的探测机制来验证引力子的存在。

Method: 采用带电谐振子阵列与动态电磁场相互作用的哈密顿量模型，分析超冷原子系统中引力子的布居反转过程，研究玻色子数目和物质波包压缩对指数增长的影响。

Result: 引力子的指数增长严格依赖于系统中的玻色子数目以及物质波包的内禀压缩程度，相干引力子源可提供引力子存在的直接证据。

Conclusion: 基于该分析，提出了产生真正的引力子激光的实验方案，为引力子的有效探测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In our recent work, arXiv:2604.11474 [hep-th], we have shown that effective detection of gravitons is possible using an array of charged harmonic oscillators in a dynamical electromagnetic field. Using the interaction Hamiltonian of the identical model, we find out that a systematic way of population inversion of the gravitons is possible in ultra-cold atomic systems. We find out that the exponential growth depends strictly on the number of bosons in the system as well as their inherent squeezing of the matter wave packets. A coherent source of gravitons may lead directly to an unavoidable evidence on the existence of gravitons and based on this analysis we propose an experimental proposal for generating true graviton laser.

</details>


### [174] [Supersymmetric twists in twistor space and holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02145)
*Matheus Balisa,Eduardo Casali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在扭量空间中计算了超对称场论的若干扭转，包括最小超对称扭转和手征代数扭转（用于超对称自对偶杨-米尔斯理论），以及N=1自对偶超引力的最小扭转。在N=4情形下，还发现了它们在手征全息框架中的全息对偶。研究表明，扭量空间中规范理论的最小扭转将其局域化到时空，显式体现了复结构的选择，并复现了时空上的最小扭转。对于超共形理论，进一步施加扭转将理论局域化到时空中的一个平面上，复现了N=4 sYM的手征代数扭转。这些扭转的体对偶也发生局域化，复现了扭曲全息的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扭量空间中研究超对称场论的扭转，建立与时空上扭转的对应关系，并在手征全息框架下寻找其全息对偶。

Method: 在扭量空间中计算超对称场论的各种扭转（最小扭转、手征代数扭转），分析其局域化行为，并与时空上的已知结果进行对比；对于N=4情形，进一步在手征全息框架中寻找全息对偶。

Result: 扭量空间中规范理论的最小扭转将其局域化到时空，复现了时空上的最小扭转；超共形理论的进一步扭转将其局域化到时空中的平面，复现了N=4 sYM的手征代数扭转；体对偶也相应局域化，复现了扭曲全息的结果。

Conclusion: 扭量空间中的扭转与时空上的扭转存在精确对应关系，且在手征全息框架下可找到对应的全息对偶，为扭曲全息提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We compute some supersymmetric twists of field theories in twistor space, including the minimal supersymmetric and the chiral algebra twists of supersymmetric self-dual Yang-Mills, and the minimal twist of $\mathcal{N}=1$ self-dual supergravity. In the case of $\mathcal{N}=4$ we also find their holographic duals in the framework of chiral holography. We find that the minimal twist of gauge theories in twistor space localizes them to spacetime, making the choice of complex structure manifest, and reproducing the minimal twist on spacetime. For superconformal theories we apply a further twist which localizes the theory to a plane contained on spacetime, reproducing the chiral algebra twist of $\mathcal{N}=4$ sYM. We show that the bulk duals of these twists also localize reproducing the results from twisted holography.

</details>


### [175] [Black Holes and Random Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02233)
*Eric Perlmutter*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Fyodorov-Hiary-Keating猜想推广到量子引力中的黑洞微观态计数问题，通过全息对偶推导出共形场论中高维算子的区间计数严格界限，并证明这些计数的极端涨落由具有指定尾部分布的随机变量刻画。在大N极限下，这些阶数为1的不规则涨落对半经典AdS引力路径积分的分辨率设定了定量极限。研究表明AdS黑洞微观态谱及其场论对偶应表现出随机矩阵的极端值统计，属于高斯对数相关场的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将随机矩阵理论中Fyodorov-Hiary-Keating猜想的极端值统计推广到量子引力与全息对偶领域，探究黑洞微观态计数与高维CFT算子的统计行为，揭示半经典引力路径积分的分辨率极限。

Method: 通过全息对偶框架将黑洞微观态计数映射为CFT高维算子的区间计数问题，利用高斯对数相关场的极端值统计理论，建立FHK猜想的引力版本，并引入高斯随机模型描述态计数的涨落行为。

Result: 推导出CFT高维初级算子区间计数的严格界限；证明态计数的极端涨落由具有规定尾部分布的随机变量控制；阐明大N下阶数为1的不规则涨落对半经典引力路径积分分辨率的定量限制；揭示了AdS/CFT中N依赖的不规则性源于高斯随机模型的去相关性质。

Conclusion: AdS黑洞微观态谱及其CFT对偶应属于随机矩阵极端值统计的普适类——高斯对数相关场，这为理解量子引力中的微观态统计行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We formulate an avatar of the Fyodorov-Hiary-Keating conjecture for black hole microstate counts in quantum gravity. By holography, this implies sharp bounds on interval counts of high-dimension primary operators in conformal field theory. The extremal fluctuations of these counts are characterized by a random variable, with a prescribed tail distribution. At large $N$, these order-one erratic fluctuations set a quantitative limit on the resolution of the semiclassical AdS gravitational path integral. Gaussian random models for state counts arise naturally in this context; we express the phenomenon of erratic $N$-dependence in AdS/CFT as a decorrelation property of these models. Our broader point is to suggest that AdS black hole microstate spectra and their field theory duals should exhibit the extreme value statistics of random matrices, lying in the universality class of Gaussian log-correlated fields.

</details>


### [176] [Boundary observables in string field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02454)
*Klaus Kaja,Carlo Maccaferri,Ulisses Portugal,Jakub Vošmera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从自由弦场论的规范不变作用量出发，定义了类似于广义相对论中Brown-York荷的规范不变可观测量，这些可观测量来源于边界tadpole，与SFT规范群的等距性相关，并在开弦和闭弦场论中给出具体例子（如电磁场解的通量和Coulomb荷、二维弦论中带弦毛黑洞的守恒荷），同时推广到相互作用弦场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由弦场论中构造类似广义相对论Brown-York荷的边界可观测量，以建立弦场论中的边界守恒荷概念，并推广到有源背景和相互作用理论。

Method: 从2506.05969中建立的带边界的自由弦场论规范不变作用量出发，定义源自边界tadpole的规范不变可观测量，这些可观测量与SFT规范群围绕给定背景的等距性相关联；一致性要求背景运动方程仅在边界上满足，从而允许在体中有源的背景下定义这些可观测量。

Result: 在开弦场论中计算了恒定电磁场强度解的边界通量和Coulomb解对应的荷；在闭弦场论中刻画了二维弦论中带弦毛黑洞解对应的无穷多守恒荷。

Conclusion: 建立了弦场论中与Brown-York荷类似的边界可观测量构造，并成功将其推广到全相互作用弦场论中。

Abstract: Starting from the gauge invariant action for free string field theory with boundary recently constructed in 2506.05969, we define new gauge invariant observables which are analogous to the Brown-York charges of General Relativity. Just like the Brown-York charges, our observables originate from a boundary tadpole, and are associated to isometries of the SFT gauge group around a given background. The consistency of the construction requires the equation of motion of the background to be satisfied only at the boundary and therefore these observables can also be defined for backgrounds generated by sources in the bulk. As examples of our construction in open string field theory, we compute the flux through the boundary of constant electromagnetic field-strength solutions and the charge associated to the Coulomb solution. As a further example in closed string field theory, we characterize the infinite conserved charges associated to stringy-haired black-hole solutions in two-dimensional string theory. We also construct a generalization of these boundary observables to the full interacting string field theory.

</details>


### [177] [BRST-BV approach to fields in Poincare patch of AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02495)
*R. R. Metsaev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用AdS空间的庞加莱参数化，发展了一种适用于自由场的通用BRST-BV方法，得到了具有任意质量和对称性类型的场的BRST-BV拉格朗日量的一般表达式，并将其应用于全对称无质量、有质量、部分有质量及连续自旋场，同时展示了与度规类拉格朗日量的匹配以及AdS空间对称性的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为AdS空间中的自由场建立一套通用的BRST-BV形式体系，统一处理不同质量和自旋类型的场，特别是连续自旋场这类特殊场。

Method: 采用AdS空间的庞加莱参数化，构建通用BRST-BV方法；导出一般表达式后，分别应用于全对称无质量、有质量、部分有质量整数自旋场及连续自旋场；分别发展受约束和不受约束的BRST-BV形式；通过与修正de Donder散度的度规类拉格朗日量进行匹配验证一致性。

Result: 得到了适用于任意质量和对称性类型的自由场的BRST-BV拉格朗日量的一般表达式；成功应用于多种自旋场（无质量、有质量、部分有质量、连续自旋）；建立了受约束和不受约束两种BRST-BV形式；证明了与度规类拉格朗日量的匹配；在场与反场空间中实现了AdS空间对称性。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用BRST-BV方法有效统一了AdS空间中各类自由场的描述，为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We use the Poincare parametrization of AdS space to develop a general BRST-BV approach for free fields. A general expression for the BRST-BV Lagrangian of fields with arbitrary masses and symmetry types is obtained. We apply this general framework to study totally symmetric massless, massive, and partially-massless fields with arbitrary integer spin and a continuous-spin field. For these fields, both the constrained and unconstrained BRST-BV formulations are developed. In addition, we demonstrate the matching between the obtained BRST-BV Lagrangian and the metric-like Lagrangian formulated in terms of the modified de Donder divergence. Finally, a realization of AdS space symmetries is obtained within the space of fields and antifields entering the BRST-BV formulation.

</details>
